GSTEP constructs a token-level spatio-temporal density by combining a continuous temporal density, obtained from a smoothed centered frame-level change signal, with intra-frame spatial density, and then performs global token sampling by jointly balancing information density and coverage. Extensive experiments on multiple VideoLLMs and public benchmarks demonstrate that GSTEP consistently achieves strong accuracy-efficiency trade-offs and generalizes well across model architectures and evaluation settings. On LLaVA-OneVision-7B, GSTEP prunes 75% of visual tokens, preserves up to 100.2% of the original average performance across benchmarks, and achieves a 1.17 end-to-end speedup.

</details>


### [31] [SUV: Future Scene Understanding as Video Generation for End-to-End Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03084)
*Yibo Yuan,Jiacheng Fu,Jiangtong Zhu,Yi Li,Jianhua Han,Meng Tian,Zhuohan Liu,Zhiwei Xiong,Hang Xu,Jianwu Fang,Jianru Xue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SUV是一个统一的端到端驾驶框架，将未来场景理解视为视频生成任务，利用预训练的视频基础模型来预测未来外观、语义、相对深度和实例级动态，并通过联合视频-动作注意力生成自车轨迹，在多个基准上取得了领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端驾驶方法使用任务特定的预测头和输出格式来建模未来场景，可扩展性有限。作者探索视频生成是否可以作为统一的预测器，从而更高效地建模未来场景。

Method: SUV框架将未来场景理解转化为视频生成，使用预训练的视频基础模型作为共享的视频专家，同时预测未来外观、语义、相对深度和实例级动态四个视频流，无需每个流的特定预测头。通过联合视频-动作注意力机制，动作专家关注所有未来流的潜在表示并生成自车轨迹。

Result: SUV直接预测所有四个未来流，结构化未来监督和直接未来流访问提高了轨迹规划得分。仅使用单个前置摄像头且无需候选轨迹选择，SUV在NAVSIM-v2的navtest上达到91.0 EPDMS，navhard上达到36.9，在WOD-E2E基准上获得7.94 RFS，超越了多个最新方法。

Conclusion: 视频生成可以作为端到端驾驶中未来场景理解的有效统一预测器，提供结构化未来监督能够提升驾驶规划性能。

Abstract: End-to-end driving requires a coherent understanding of future scenes, yet existing methods model these scenes using task-specific heads and output formats, with limited scalability. Can video generation instead provide a shared predictor? We introduce SUV, a unified end-to-end driving framework that casts future Scene Understanding as Video generation using a pretrained video foundation model. SUV models future appearance, semantics, relative depth, and instance-level dynamics as video streams with a shared video expert, without stream-specific visual prediction heads. Through joint video-action attention, the action expert attends to the latent representations of all future streams and generates the ego trajectory. Experiments show that SUV directly predicts all four future streams, while controlled ablations show that structured future supervision and direct future-stream access yield higher trajectory planning scores. With only a single front camera and no candidate-trajectory selection, SUV outperforms a broad set of recent state-of-the-art methods on both NAVSIM-v2 splits, achieving 91.0 EPDMS on navtest and 36.9 on navhard. On the long-tail WOD-E2E benchmark, SUV achieves a competitive RFS of 7.94.

</details>


### [32] [Channel-wise Dynamic Knowledge Distillation via Adaptive Sample Generation for Action Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03100)
*Ping Li,Chenhao Ping,Jie Song,Mingli Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自适应样本感知的通道动态知识蒸馏方法（ASCD KD），用于压缩大型动作识别模型。该方法通过自适应样本生成模块创建更新样本，并利用通道动态蒸馏模块训练学生网络，在多个视频和图像基准上取得了最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识蒸馏方法存在两个关键局限：1）依赖固定输入样本导致冻结教师网络与可学习学生网络之间的特征对齐次优；2）对所有通道施加统一的蒸馏强度，未能考虑它们在不同训练周期中捕获不同知识（如运动节奏或幅度）的重要性变化。

Method: 提出的ASCD KD方法包含两个阶段：首先，通过自适应样本生成模块，利用样本梯度（通过最小化由通道质心频率差异加权的特征损失得到）创建更新样本，同时使用高斯掩码保留频率特征中关键的运动相关细节；其次，采用通道动态蒸馏模块，在生成样本上基于样本梯度和特征频率指导学生网络训练。样本定期更新以提高效率。

Result: 在UCF101、Kinetics-400、Something-Something-v2三个视频基准以及CIFAR-100、ImageNet两个图像数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 提出的自适应样本感知通道动态蒸馏方法有效克服了现有KD方法的局限，在动作识别模型压缩中展现了优越性。

Abstract: Knowledge Distillation (KD) offers a promising yet underexplored path for compressing large action recognition models. However, existing KD methods suffer from two key limitations: 1) reliance on fixed input samples leads to suboptimal feature alignment between the frozen teacher (larger model) and the learnable student (smaller model), and 2) applying a uniform distillation strength for all channels fails to account for their varying importance in capturing distinct knowledge (e.g., motion tempo or magnitude) across training epochs. This motivates us to develop an Adaptive Sample-aware Channel-wise Dynamic (ASCD) KD approach, which operates in two stages. First, we use an adaptive sample generation module to create updated samples by incorporating semantics from sample gradients, which are derived by minimizing a feature loss weighted by channel centroid frequency differences at each layer. Meanwhile, crucial motion-related details are preserved by applying a Gaussian mask to frequency features. Second, we employ a channel-wise dynamic distillation module to train student on these generated samples, guided by sample gradients and feature frequencies. For efficiency, samples are updated periodically rather than per epoch. Extensive experiments on three video benchmarks (UCF101, Kinetics-400, Something-Something-v2) and two image datasets (CIFAR-100, ImageNet) demonstrate the state-of-the-art performance of our method. Code is available at https://github.com/mlvccn/ASCD_KD_Action.

</details>


### [33] [Double Down on Defense: Strengthening Deep Perceptual Hashes against Evasion Attacks without Retraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03101)
*Bangjie Sun,Nayoung Kim,Mun Choon Chan,Jun Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DualShield 是一种即插即用的防御方法，通过匹配时的随机平滑和发布前的参考图像硬化，提升现有深度感知哈希的鲁棒性，无需重新训练或修改模型，在多个哈希算法和数据集上显著降低攻击成功率并保持低碰撞率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度感知哈希被广泛应用于近似重复图像匹配，但对抗性扰动可导致匹配失效。现有方法需重新训练或修改模型，成本高昂。本文旨在不重训练的前提下提升哈希鲁棒性。

Method: DualShield 包含两个机制：1) 匹配时随机平滑（在查询-参考对聚合决策）；2) 发布前硬化（为参考图像添加优化的不可察觉扰动）。二者结合提供认证和经验的鲁棒性。

Result: DualShield 实现了约 0.3 的认证 ℓ2 半径，在该半径内查询扰动无法逃避匹配。在八种深度感知哈希和三个数据集上，它显著降低了自适应白盒、黑盒和图像变换攻击的成功率，同时保持低碰撞率。

Conclusion: 深度感知哈希可以通过改进匹配流程和发布前硬化参考图像来增强鲁棒性，无需昂贵的重新训练。DualShield 提供了一种有效的即插即用防御方案。

Abstract: Near-duplicate image matching is crucial for trust and safety, provenance verification, copyright enforcement, and large-scale visual search. Modern platforms increasingly rely on deep perceptual hashes, which map visually similar images to nearby representations despite common image transformations. However, adversarial perturbations can cause near-duplicates to evade matching. We present DualShield, a plug-in defense that improves the robustness of existing deep perceptual hashes without retraining or modifying their underlying models. DualShield combines matching-time randomized smoothing, which aggregates decisions over perturbed reference-query pairs, with publication-time hardening, which adds an optimized imperceptible perturbation to each reference image before publication. Together, these mechanisms provide certified and empirical robustness. DualShield achieves a certified $\ell_2$ radius of approximately 0.3, guaranteeing that query perturbations within this radius cannot evade matching. We further evaluate it against adaptive white-box, black-box, and image-transformation attacks. Across eight deep perceptual hashes and three datasets, DualShield substantially reduces attack success rates while preserving low collision rates. These results show that deep perceptual hashes can be strengthened without costly retraining by improving the matching procedure and hardening reference images before publication.

</details>


### [34] [FaithIR: Rethinking Infrared Image Super-Resolution from Perceptual Sharpness to Task Relevant Fidelity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03106)
*Axi Niu,Zhenguo Wu,Kang Zhang,Qingsen Yan,Jinqiu Sun,Yanning Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FaithIR，一个忠实于原始红外结构与语义信息的超分辨率框架，旨在提升下游机器感知任务（目标检测、语义分割）的可靠性，而非追求视觉锐利度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有红外图像超分辨率方法常产生人工纹理、过锐边缘和虚假高频细节，扭曲了真实的红外热结构及语义信息，影响下游感知任务的可靠性。

Method: FaithIR包含两个分支：补丁级条件分支捕获全局热和结构信息，像素级恢复分支在结构指导下进行密集局部重建。整个恢复过程直接在像素域进行，以保留红外特有结构和任务相关信息。

Result: 在FLIR-IISR、M3FD和FMB数据集上，FaithIR展现出强重建保真度、跨数据集泛化能力，并在目标检测和语义分割任务中取得优越性能。

Conclusion: 保留忠实的红外结构信息对于可靠的机器感知比单纯追求感知锐度更为重要。

Abstract: Infrared image super-resolution (IISR) is important for downstream tasks such as object detection and semantic segmentation. Existing IISR methods often produce artificial textures, over-sharpened edges, and spurious high-frequency details that distort authentic thermal structures and semantic information. To address this issue, we propose FaithIR, a faithful infrared super-resolution framework for reliable machine perception. FaithIR consists of a patch-level conditioning branch that captures global thermal and structural information and a pixel-level restoration branch that performs dense local reconstruction under structural guidance. The entire restoration process is performed directly in the pixel domain to preserve infrared-specific structures and task-relevant information. Extensive experiments on FLIR-IISR, M3FD, and FMB demonstrate strong reconstruction fidelity, cross-dataset generalization, and superior performance in object detection and semantic segmentation. These results show that demonstrate that preserving faithful infrared structure preservations is more important for reliable machine perception than merely pursuing perceptual sharpness alone.

</details>


### [35] [A Unified Resolution-Conditioned Framework for Orthogonal Line-Scanning Image Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03107)
*Yiming Gong,Kai Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于Rank Enhanced Linear Attention (RELA)的统一分辨率条件融合框架，通过特征线性调制(FiLM)和自适应RELA机制，使单个模型能够适应不同狭缝宽度的激光线扫描显微镜配置，实现接近各向同性的分辨率重建，在多种配置下达到34-40 dB PSNR，并平滑泛化到未见过的中间配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激光线扫描显微镜虽然能够快速体积成像，但会产生各向异性的横向分辨率。正交线扫描可以提供互补的方向信息来恢复接近各向同性的分辨率，然而现有的深度学习方法需要为每种光学配置单独训练模型，缺乏统一性和泛化能力。

Method: 提出统一的分辨率条件融合框架：(1) 基于Rank Enhanced Linear Attention (RELA)架构；(2) 使用特征线性调制(FiLM)将网络连续地条件化在分辨能力比率上，使单一模型适应不同狭缝宽度；(3) 引入自适应RELA，用比率条件的多尺度深度卷积替代固定核秩增强，并使用可学习的注意力温度来根据退化严重程度调整选择性；(4) 利用物理基础的可分离点扩散函数模型生成多狭缝配置的训练数据，并通过实测光学数据验证（48.3 dB精度）。

Result: 模型在多种狭缝配置下达到34-40 dB PSNR，而无条件的多狭缝训练仅达到24.3 dB，单狭缝专业模型在训练配置外损失4-9 dB。模型能平滑泛化到未见过的中间配置。消融实验表明：FiLM解决了配置模糊性，全局线性注意力捕获了长距离方向对应关系，自适应温度在具有挑战性的近各向同性区域额外提升了2 dB。

Conclusion: 提出的统一框架能够用一个模型适应多种光学配置，实现了接近各向同性的分辨率重建，并且具有良好的泛化能力，避免了为每种配置单独训练模型的繁琐，为激光线扫描显微镜的图像融合提供了高效、统一的解决方案。

Abstract: Laser line-scanning microscopy enables fast volumetric imaging but produces anisotropic lateral resolution. Orthogonal line scans provide complementary directional information that can recover near-isotropic resolution, yet existing deep-learning methods require a separate model for each optical configuration. We present a unified, resolution-conditioned fusion framework based on Rank Enhanced Linear Attention (RELA). Feature-wise Linear Modulation (FiLM) conditions the network continuously on the resolving-power ratio, enabling one model to adapt across slit widths. We further introduce Adaptive RELA, which replaces fixed-kernel rank enhancement with ratio-conditioned multi-scale depthwise convolutions and uses a learnable attention temperature to adjust selectivity with degradation severity. Training data spanning multiple slit configurations are generated using a physics-grounded separable point-spread-function model verified against measured optical data at 48.3 dB accuracy. The resulting model achieves 34-40 dB PSNR across configurations, whereas unconditioned multi-slit training collapses to 24.3 dB and per-slit specialists lose 4-9 dB outside their training setting. It also generalizes smoothly to unseen intermediate configurations without interpolation artifacts. Ablations show that FiLM resolves configuration ambiguity, global linear attention captures long-range directional correspondences, and adaptive temperature yields an additional 2 dB in the challenging near-isotropic regime, where complementary signals are weak.

</details>


### [36] [Multimodal Plant Root Phenotyping with Integration of 3D Skeleton Extraction and Language Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03109)
*Jiakai Lin,Zijun Li,Guoyu Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种多模态机器人AI框架，结合3D骨架提取（加权拉普拉斯收缩W-LBC）与语言引导推理，实现可解释且数据高效的植物根系表型分析。通过无监督骨架提取生成高保真结构表征，计算形态描述子，并利用证据优先语言建模微调GPT为交互式分析聊天机器人，在12种植物上验证了鲁棒且可解释的推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 植物根系表型分析对于理解地下结构、优化作物管理及提高农业可持续性至关重要，但传统方法缺乏可解释性和数据效率。本文旨在通过融合3D几何感知与大语言模型，建立定量分析与语义解释之间的桥梁，实现可解释的机器人根系表型分析。

Method: 1) 基于加权拉普拉斯收缩（W-LBC）的无监督骨架提取网络，从机器人3D传感平台获取的稠密点云生成高保真结构表示；2) 从重建骨架图计算定量形态描述子（根数量、长度、分支角度、密度）；3) 证据优先语言建模框架，使用自动生成的指令-响应对微调GPT作为交互式分析聊天机器人，每个训练样本在自然语言推理前提供可测量的证据。

Result: 该结构引导框架在12种具有不同根系构型的植物物种上实现了鲁棒且可解释的推理，能够产生生物学一致的生长模式和适应性特征解释。

Conclusion: 通过集成无监督3D几何感知与大语言模型理解，该方法弥合了定量分析与语义解释之间的鸿沟，为可解释的机器人植物根系表型分析建立了统一范式。

Abstract: Plant root phenotyping is fundamental to understanding below-ground structures, optimizing crop management, and improving agricultural sustainability. This paper presents a multimodal robotic AI framework that integrates 3D skeleton extraction with language-guided reasoning for interpretable and data-efficient root analysis. We develop an unsupervised skeleton extraction network based on Weighted Laplacian Contraction (W-LBC) to generate high-fidelity structural representations from dense point clouds captured by robotic 3D sensing platforms. Quantitative morphological descriptors, including root count, length, branching angle, and density, are computed from the reconstructed skeleton graph to capture geometric and topological characteristics. Building on these features, we introduce an Evidence-First language modeling framework that fine-tunes GPT as an interactive analytical chatbot using automatically generated instruction--response pairs. Each training sample provides measurable evidence before natural-language reasoning, enabling the model to ground interpretation in quantitative morphology. Through supervised fine-tuning, GPT associates numerical structure with semantic meaning, producing biologically consistent explanations of growth patterns and adaptive traits. Experiments show that the structure-guided framework achieves robust, interpretable reasoning across 12 plant species with diverse root architectures. By integrating unsupervised 3D geometric perception with large-scale language understanding, our approach bridges quantitative analysis and semantic interpretation, establishing a unified paradigm for explainable robotic plant root phenotyping.

</details>


### [37] [Adaptive Two-Stage Visual Token Pruning for Efficient Inference in Video-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03112)
*Paribesh Regmi,Qingshuang Chen,Chi Zhang,Heba Aly,Yelin Kim,Hongda Mao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需额外训练的两阶段自适应标记剪枝策略，用于加速视频视觉语言模型推理。第一阶段剪除冗余帧，第二阶段根据内容自适应调整标记剪枝比例，在保留10%标记的情况下，视频字幕任务准确率提升7%，计算量减少95%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有标记剪枝技术主要针对单图像输入，未考虑视频中的时间冗余和帧间冗余，且采用固定的统一剪枝比例，无法适应不同视频内容中冗余度的变化，导致在资源受限设备和实时监控场景中部署困难。

Method: 提出两阶段自适应标记剪枝策略：第一阶段剪除冗余帧；第二阶段在保留帧内进行标记级剪枝，剪枝比例通过分析标记嵌入的相关性结构来量化冗余度，从而根据视频内容自适应确定。该方法完全事后（post-hoc），无需额外训练或微调。

Result: 在视频字幕基准上，仅保留10%标记时准确率提升7%，计算量（TFLOPs）减少95%。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了视频模型中标记冗余问题，通过自适应剪枝在保证性能的同时大幅降低计算开销，适用于边缘设备和实时监控等场景。

Abstract: Vision-language models excel at image and video understanding but suffer from high inference latency due to the need to process thousands of tokens per image, limiting their deployment on resource-constrained edge devices and in real-time surveillance applications. This challenge is further amplified in video processing, where multiple frames must be analyzed simultaneously. Existing token reduction techniques are largely developed for single-image inputs and therefore fail to account for the temporal and inter-frame redundancies present in video sequences. In addition, these methods generally rely on a fixed, uniform pruning ratio applied across all inputs, which is suboptimal because the degree of redundancy can vary significantly between different videos, necessitating content-dependent pruning levels to preserve critical information. To address these limitations, we propose a two-stage adaptive token pruning strategy specifically designed for video processing. In the first stage, we prune out the redundant frames, and in the second stage, token-level pruning is applied within the retained frames. Crucially, the pruning ratio in the second stage is determined adaptively based on the content of each video. This is achieved by analyzing the correlation structure of token embeddings to quantify redundancy, which is used to determine the ratio. Importantly, our method is entirely post-hoc and requires no additional training or fine-tuning, while achieving strong empirical gains; notably, it improves accuracy by +7\% on a video captioning benchmark at 10\% token retention, while reducing computation TFLOPs by 95\%.

</details>


### [38] [Non-Destructive Quantification of Urea Adulteration in Bovine Milk Using Transmittance Multispectral Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03113)
*Sharukshan Niranjan,Iresha Ranaweera,Tharindu Chandrarathne,Kalana Dissanayaka,Roshan Godaliyadda,Vijitha Herath,Parakrama Ekanayake,Janak Vidanarachchi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于多光谱成像（MSI）的框架，用于在受控密度条件下定量检测牛奶中的尿素掺假。通过自建的12波段多光谱成像系统（365–940 nm）采集样品图像，结合多元线性回归（验证R²=0.9599）和前馈神经网络（验证R²=0.9773），实现了准确、非破坏性的尿素定量分析，为牛奶质量快速筛查提供了低成本、有效的替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 牛奶中尿素掺假是重要的食品质量和健康问题，亟需快速、定量的筛查工具。现有方法（实验室分析、光谱技术等）因需要专业设备、样品制备、化学试剂或实验室操作，难以满足快速、低成本的常规筛查需求。

Method: 采用自建的多光谱成像系统（12个离散波段，365–940 nm）采集掺入尿素并用水平衡密度的牛奶样品图像；通过比重计验证牛奶在20°C时比重为1.032；使用多元线性回归和前馈神经网络建立预测模型。

Result: 多元线性回归的验证R²为0.9599，前馈神经网络的验证R²提升至0.9773，表明该方法在受控密度平衡条件下能准确、非破坏性地定量尿素。

Conclusion: 透射多光谱成像技术在受控密度平衡条件下可准确、非破坏性地定量牛奶中的尿素，具有作为牛奶质量快速筛查方法的潜力。

Abstract: Adulteration of bovine milk using urea remains a major food quality and health concern, motivating the development of rapid and quantitative screening tools. Conventional approaches, including laboratory-based analytical methods and spectroscopic techniques, have been used for urea detection; however, many remain less suitable for rapid, low-cost routine screening due to requirements such as specialized instrumentation, sample preparation, chemical reagents, or laboratory operation. This study introduces a pragmatic, cost-effective, accurate, and laboratory-validated MSI-based method for quantitative urea estimation under controlled density conditions using a multispectral-imaging-based regression framework. An in-house-built multispectral imaging system operating in twelve discrete spectral bands (365--940~nm) was used to acquire multispectral images of milk samples prepared with controlled urea addition and water for density balancing. Fresh milk was obtained on the day of image acquisition, and the specific gravity of the milk was verified to be 1.032 at 20°C using a hydrometer. Multiple linear regression provided an initial mapping with a high validation $R^2$ of 0.9599, while a feed-forward neural network further improved predictive performance with a validation $R^2$ of 0.9773. These results demonstrate the feasibility of transmittance multispectral imaging for accurate, non-destructive urea quantification under controlled density-balanced conditions, supporting its potential as a rapid screening approach for milk-quality assessment.

</details>


### [39] [SeCo-SBIR: Semantically Consistent Prompt Learning for Zero-Shot Sketch-Based Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03120)
*Long Hoang Dang,Tuan Nguyen Huu,Nguyen Minh Hieu,Tu Minh Phuong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SeCo-SBIR 提出了一种语义一致的提示学习框架，通过文本引导的多模态提示和扰动一致性约束，在保持 CLIP 零样本泛化能力的同时适应草图-照片域间隙，在三个标准 ZS-SBIR 基准上达到最先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通过提示学习适配 CLIP 进行零样本草图检索（ZS-SBIR）时面临根本矛盾：模型需要桥接草图-照片的领域差异（需要任务特定适配），但增加的灵活性容易使模型过拟合到训练时的可见类别，从而侵蚀 CLIP 原有的零样本泛化能力。

Method: 提出 SeCo-SBIR 框架，包含两个核心机制：(1) 文本引导的多模态提示策略——可学习提示向量经 CLIP 文本编码器处理后，通过可学习耦合函数将各层中间表示注入视觉编码器每一层，利用文本编码器已学习的鲁棒语义知识实现领域适配并保持泛化；(2) 基于扰动的约束——采用非对称 InfoNCE 目标，将增强输入送入冻结的 CLIP 参考分支，干净输入送入可训练分支，将习得表示锚定到 CLIP 泛化特征空间。此外结合轻量适配器和多目标损失（三元组、NT-Xent、分类损失）。

Result: 在类别级、广义级和跨数据集三种零样本草图检索基准设置下，SeCo-SBIR 在所有三个标准 ZS-SBIR 基准上均取得了最先进的结果。

Conclusion: 所提出的语义一致提示学习框架成功解决了领域适配与零样本泛化之间的固有矛盾，通过注入可迁移语义知识和约束表示锚定，既实现了草图-照片的领域桥接，又保留了 CLIP 的强泛化能力。

Abstract: Adapting CLIP for zero-shot sketch-based image retrieval (ZS-SBIR) via prompt learning faces a fundamental tension: the model must bridge the sketch-photo domain gap through task-specific adaptation, yet the added flexibility risks overfitting to seen training categories and eroding CLIP's zero-shot generalization. We present SeCo-SBIR, a semantically consistent prompt learning framework that resolves this tension from both sides. First, a text-guided multi-modal prompting strategy routes learnable prompt vectors through CLIP's text encoder and projects the resulting intermediate representations into the visual encoder at every layer via learnable coupling functions. Because the text encoder has already learned robust, abstract category-level semantics from large-scale language supervision, this mechanism injects transferable semantic knowledge directly into the visual pathway - adapting the model to the sketch-photo domain while inherently favoring generalization to unseen classes. Second, a perturbation-based consistency constraint addresses the residual overfitting risk from the learnable coupling functions by aligning the adapted model with a frozen CLIP reference branch using an asymmetric InfoNCE objective - augmented inputs feed the frozen branch while clean inputs feed the trainable branch - anchoring the learned representations to CLIP's generalizable feature space. Together with lightweight adapters and a multi-objective loss combining triplet, NT-Xent, and classification terms, SeCo-SBIR achieves state-of-the-art results on all three standard ZS-SBIR benchmarks across categorical, generalized, and across-dataset settings.

</details>


### [40] [Rectify Then Diffuse: Disentangling Concepts Before Denoising Trajectory Unfolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03135)
*Ning Zhu,An Chen,Mengfei Zhao,Juntao Xu,Jingze Liang,Boyuan Gu,Liang-Jian Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Rectify-then-Diffuse (RTD)框架，通过一次性修正初始潜变量分配（而非持续干预去噪轨迹）来解决文本到图像扩散模型中的概念组合失败问题。RTD包含两个轻量级、无需训练的模块：Soft-Overlap Disentanglement (SOD)将概念图间的归一化重叠转化为可微分的分离目标，Isotropic Gradient Rectification (IGR)归一化梯度并施加有界潜变量位移。在AE-Bench物体对子集上，RTD相比CO3提升BLIP-VQA 45.8%、ImageReward 19.6%，且速度快2.3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在组合生成时经常将不同概念错误合并（如"一个红苹果和一个蓝香蕉"可能生成红香蕉），已有方法（如注意力控制、重采样）要么需要训练，要么计算开销大。本文追溯到前期协调瓶颈（early coordination bottleneck）：在去噪开始前，提示条件注意力已将不同概念分配到高度重叠的空间区域，导致后续去噪过程中注意力相互耦合无法解耦。

Method: 提出训练无关的RTD框架，在标准去噪前一次性修正初始潜变量分配。具体包括：(1) Soft-Overlap Disentanglement (SOD)：将概念图间的归一化重叠转化为可微分、布局无关的分离目标；(2) Isotropic Gradient Rectification (IGR)：对SOD梯度进行各向同性归一化，施加有界潜变量位移，确保跨提示和初始化的尺度一致性。仅需一次修正即可改善初始注意力分配。

Result: 在AE-Bench物体对子集上，RTD相比CO3提升BLIP-VQA 45.8%、ImageReward 19.6%，速度提升2.3倍。在多个组合生成基准上取得最先进组成保真度，且无需训练，泛化性强。

Conclusion: 将组合生成重新定义为边界条件问题（一次性修正初始分配）而非持续控制演化轨迹，更高效且有效。揭示了扩散模型组合失败的根本原因在于早期坐标瓶颈，为未来无需训练的组成改进提供了新方向。

Abstract: Text-to-image diffusion models can generate individual concepts well, but they often omit or merge concepts incorrectly with multiple concepts. We trace these failures to an early coordination bottleneck: before denoising begins, prompt-conditioned attention may allocate different concepts to strongly overlapping spatial support, which can keep their attention coupled as denoising proceeds. This observation motivates treating compositional generation as a boundary-condition problem rather than repeatedly controlling the evolving trajectory. To this end, we propose Rectify-then-Diffuse (RTD), a training-free framework that rectifies the initial allocation once before standard denoising. Firstly, we propose Soft-Overlap Disentanglement (SOD), which converts normalized overlap between pilot concept maps into a differentiable and layout-agnostic separation objective. Secondly, we introduce Isotropic Gradient Rectification (IGR), which normalizes the SOD gradient and applies a bounded latent displacement with a consistent scale across prompts and initializations. Extensive experiments show that RTD achieves state-of-the-art compositional fidelity and robust gains. On the AE-Bench object pair subset, RTD improves BLIP-VQA by 45.8% and ImageReward by 19.6% over CO3 while running 2.3$\times$ faster. Code will be released at https://github.com/Z-yiwei/rectify-then-diffuse

</details>


### [41] [Frozen High-Resolution Inference for Cross-City Object Detection: An AI City Challenge 2026 Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03136)
*Jaeuk Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了跨城市目标检测中，在仅有聚合COCO AP反馈（混合源城市和目标城市图像）的封闭训练平台内，通过增大推理分辨率（从704×704冻结模型提升至1120×1120）即可显著提升聚合AP（+0.0382），而无需任何参数更新。实验表明，域内验证AP不可靠，且灰度世界归一化、微调等方法未能超越简单的高分辨率冻结推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨城市目标检测需要检测器在未标注的目标城市中泛化，但实际中常面临目标城市无标签且反馈仅为聚合指标（混合源和目标城市）的受限场景。作者旨在分析在这种“空气间隙”的Training-as-a-Service平台中，不同配置（尤其是推理分辨率调整）对聚合AP的影响，并验证域内验证指标的可靠性。

Method: 使用单一RF-DETR-Large检测器，在封闭平台中仅通过聚合COCO AP反馈进行探索。实验包括：冻结模型提升推理分辨率（从704×704到1120×1120）、热启动微调（1120px）、灰度世界归一化、以及矩形推理（但发现误用了人像方向）。所有配置仅提交一次，最佳结果由隐藏服务器选择。作者公开了平台命令、配置快照和证据边界。

Result: 冻结1120×1120推理（训练于704×704）取得最高聚合AP（0.3272→0.3654），小物体相对增益最大，中等物体绝对增益最大。热启动微调达到0.3470，但其域内验证AP上升（0.767→0.789），表明域内验证不可靠。灰度世界归一化未显著改变结果；矩形运行因误用人像方向而被否定。

Conclusion: 增大推理分辨率是简单有效的跨城市泛化方法，但聚合AP结果仅反映混合域表现，不能直接证明目标城市特定改进。域内验证指标在仅有聚合反馈时可能误导模型选择。本研究的探索性、审计性发现强调了在受限反馈下谨慎解读实验结果的必要性。

Abstract: Cross-city object detection requires a detector trained in one city to generalize to an unlabeled target city. In AI City Challenge 2026 Track 6, we analyze archived configurations of a single RF-DETR-Large detector inside an air-gapped Training-as-a-Service platform whose server returns only an aggregate COCO-style AP over a hidden mixture of source- and target-city images. Frozen 1120 x 1120 inference of a checkpoint trained at 704 x 704 achieved the highest aggregate AP among the evaluated configurations (0.3272 -> 0.3654, +0.0382) without any parameter update, with the largest relative gain on small objects and the largest absolute gain on medium objects, at 2.53x the input pixels. A warm-start 1120px fine-tuning recipe reached 0.3470 while its in-domain validation AP rose (0.767 -> 0.789), a caution that in-domain validation is an unreliable model-selection signal under aggregate-only cross-city feedback. Because that run's evaluation used a higher confidence threshold than the inference-only runs (0.05 vs. 0.01), we treat its score as a descriptive archived outcome rather than a controlled verdict on fine-tuning. Gray-world normalization did not meaningfully change the frozen-1120 result, and a rectangular run was found by audit to have used an unintended portrait orientation. We release verbatim platform commands, configuration snapshots, and an explicit evidence boundary for every claim. Each configuration was submitted once and the best was selected on the hidden server, so these are exploratory, audited findings about the aggregate mixture; they do not establish target-city-specific improvement.

</details>


### [42] [From Routes to Steps: Separating Semantic Progress from Local Execution in Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03143)
*Xiangyun Huang,Xiangchen Wang,Runfeng Lin,Yihao Xu,Kangyu Huang,Jiang Hengchen,Xiwang Dong,Lin Jiarong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Route2Step 将 VLN 中的语义进度追踪与动作生成解耦，通过显式的步骤级接口（指令分析模块 M_IA 预测进度状态，动作生成模块 M_AG 生成局部动作块）和步骤对齐程序 E-SPA（自动将子指令与演示路线段关联）来区分进度追踪错误与执行错误，在 R2R-CE 上将 SR 从 48.1% 提升至 55.3%，SPL 从 43.3% 提升至 48.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLM 导航器仅通过下一动作预测同时监督进度追踪和动作执行，导致无法区分智能体是选择了错误的子指令（进度追踪错误）还是未能正确执行当前指令（执行错误）。这种歧义使得智能体可能持续基于错误的进度状态做决策，因此需要解耦这两类能力以实现更透明的错误归因和针对性监督。

Method: 提出 Route2Step 框架，包含：(1) 指令分析模块 (M_IA) 从全局指令和视觉历史预测语义进度状态；(2) 动作生成模块 (M_AG) 基于该状态和近期观测生成局部动作块；(3) E-SPA 步骤对齐程序，自动将子指令与路线级演示的对应段关联，从而在没有人工时间标签的情况下监督进度状态，同时对重复失败于正确子指令的 rollout 组保留直接动作监督。

Result: 在 R2R-CE 上，SR 从 48.1% 提升至 55.3%（+7.2%），SPL 从 43.3% 提升至 48.2%（+4.9%）。仅需 11.5K 直接动作监督状态，配合 190K 状态级纠正样本。在真实室内外环境中进一步验证了实际适用性。

Conclusion: 通过显式解耦进度追踪与动作生成，Route2Step 有效识别并纠正了两种不同类型的导航错误，显著提升了 VLN 性能，且对人工标注的需求极低，展示了良好的可扩展性和实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) requires an agent to follow a route-level instruction by executing its constituent steps from egocentric visual observations. Existing VLM-based navigators typically supervise both capabilities through next-action prediction alone, making progress-tracking errors difficult to distinguish from execution errors. When an agent deviates from the route, a corrective action label may recover the next movement but does not indicate whether the agent selected the wrong sub-instruction or failed to execute the correct one. Consequently, the agent may continue making decisions from an erroneous progress state. To resolve this ambiguity, we propose \textbf{Route2Step}, a framework that decouples semantic progress tracking from action generation through an explicit step-level interface. The Instruction Analysis Module ($\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{IA}}$) predicts this state from the global instruction and visual history. Conditioned on the predicted state and recent observations, the Action Generation Module ($\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{AG}}$) generates local action chunks. To supervise the progress state without manual temporal labels, E-SPA, a step-alignment procedure, associates sub-instructions with their corresponding portions of route-level demonstrations. These alignments enable state supervision for incorrect progress estimates, while direct action supervision is reserved for rollout groups that repeatedly fail under the correct active sub-instruction. On R2R-CE, Route2Step improves SR from 48.1\% to 55.3\% and SPL from 43.3\% to 48.2\%, using 190K state-level corrective samples while requiring only 11.5K directly action-supervised states. Experiments in real-world indoor and outdoor environments further demonstrate the practical applicability of Route2Step. The project page is: https://sisyphus-hxy.github.io/Route2Step/.

</details>


### [43] [CROSS: Cascaded Distillation and Dual-Constraint Grounding for Remote Sensing Referring Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03147)
*Tingzhang Luo,Ruizhong Liu,Yichao Liu,Cheng Fan,Yu Liu,Jianyuan Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CROSS is a tightly integrated paradigm for Referring Remote Sensing Image Segmentation that addresses architectural weak-coupling and object-centric semantic bias via Linguistic-Guided Cascaded Distillation (LGCD) and Perspective-Spatial Contrastive Learning (PSCL), achieving state-of-the-art performance and robust localization under spatial perturbations.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing RRSIS methods suffer from two limitations: (1) Architectural Weak-Coupling, where unidirectional flow from VLMs to SAM causes coarse prompts and ignores SAM's pixel-level guidance, leading to localization drift; (2) Object-Centric Semantic Bias, where models overemphasize dominant object semantics and neglect spatial reasoning essential for RRSIS.

Method: CROSS introduces two components: (1) Linguistic-Guided Cascaded Distillation (LGCD) which distills SAM's geometric affinities as soft regularizers into VLM intermediate layers to inject dense structural priors and refine localization; (2) Perspective-Spatial Contrastive Learning (PSCL) that imposes cross-anchored constraints by mining mask-filtered deceptive distractors and spatial-linguistic counterfactuals as hard negatives to shatter semantic shortcuts and enforce logical consistency.

Result: Extensive experiments on RRSIS benchmarks demonstrate that CROSS achieves state-of-the-art performance and maintains precise localization even under severe spatial description perturbations.

Conclusion: CROSS stands as a robust new paradigm for RRSIS, effectively addressing the architectural weak-coupling and semantic bias issues through tightly integrated distillation and contrastive learning.

Abstract: Referring Remote Sensing Image Segmentation (RRSIS) has achieved significant progress through the integration of VLMs and the Segment Anything Model (SAM). However, this progress largely relies on strong pre-trained capabilities, while leaving two fundamental limitations insufficiently addressed: (1) Architectural Weak-Coupling, where the unidirectional flow forces reliance on coarse VLM prompts and wastes SAM's pixel-level structural guidance, causing localization drift; and (2) Object-Centric Semantic Bias, where models overemphasize dominant object semantics while remaining insensitive to spatial reasoning crucial for RRSIS. Motivated by these observations, we propose CROSS, a tightly integrated paradigm for RRSIS. First, we introduce Linguistic-Guided Cascaded Distillation (LGCD) to bridge the architectural gap, which distills SAM's geometric affinities as soft regularizers into VLM intermediate layers, injecting dense structural priors to refine localization. Second, Perspective-Spatial Contrastive Learning (PSCL) imposes cross-anchored constraints by mining mask-filtered deceptive distractors and spatial-linguistic counterfactuals as hard negatives, explicitly shattering semantic shortcuts to enforce genuine logical consistency. Extensive experiments on RRSIS benchmarks demonstrate that CROSS achieves state-of-the-art performance and maintains precise localization even under severe spatial description perturbations, standing as a robust new paradigm for RRSIS.

</details>


### [44] [Surface Keypoint Representation for Multi-Object and Articulated Human-Object Interaction Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03158)
*Xiaogang Peng,Zeyu Han,Zichong Meng,Yiming Xie,Jihua Zhu,Gang Hua,Huaizu Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出表面关键点轨迹作为物体运动表征，用于多物体和关节物体的全身交互生成。将交互生成分解为三个阶段：物体运动生成、接触距离场预测、全身运动合成。在多个数据集上达到或超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人-物交互生成方法大多假设与单个刚体交互，难以扩展到多物体或关节物体（如具有铰链、关节等机制）。需要一种统一的表示方法处理多物体协调和不同关节类型。

Method: 提出表面关键点轨迹（surface keypoint trajectories）作为物体运动表征，对每个刚体组件（独立物体或关节物体的一部分）跟踪少量非共线表面点。引入时空接触距离场（spatio-temporal contact distance field），将基于距离的接触建模扩展到全身、多物体和关节场景。将HOI生成分解为三个阶段：1）从文本或路径点生成物体运动；2）预测接触距离场；3）通过接触引导优化合成全身运动。

Result: 在ParaHome, HIMO, ARCTIC, OMOMO四个数据集上实验，在单物体、多物体和关节交互场景中表现优于或等同于现有方法。

Conclusion: 表面关键点轨迹是一种有效且通用的物体运动表征，能够处理多物体协调和多种关节机制，结合接触距离场可生成更自然的全身交互运动。

Abstract: Daily activities require humans to coordinate whole-body motion with the motion of surrounding objects. Despite recent progress in human-object interaction (HOI) generation, most existing methods assume interactions with a single rigid object and do not extend well to scenarios involving a variable number of objects or articulated objects with diverse joint mechanisms. We propose surface keypoint trajectories as an object motion representation: for each rigid component, whether a standalone object or one part of an articulated assembly, we track a small set of non-collinear surface points over time. This representation handles multi-object coordination and diverse articulation mechanisms directly from point dynamics without requiring explicit joint-type specification. To model when and where each body region contacts each object, we introduce a spatio-temporal contact distance field that extends distance-based contact modeling to whole-body, multi-object, and articulated settings. We factorize HOI generation into three stages: generating object motions from text or waypoints, predicting the contact distance field, and synthesizing whole-body motion with contact-guided optimization. Experiments on ParaHome, HIMO, ARCTIC, and OMOMO demonstrate better or comparable performance to existing methods across single-object, multi-object, and articulated interaction settings.

</details>


### [45] [Frequency-Decorrelated Temporal Ensembles for EEG--fNIRS Imagined-Handwriting Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03176)
*Xiao Fan,Hongbin Guo,Yubo Han,Yi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FRED is a task-adapted system for subject-independent imagined handwriting classification using EEG and fNIRS. It employs a compact multi-scale temporal network trained on three complementary EEG frequency views with a nine-member ensemble, achieving 0.7952 overall accuracy. The system incorporates transductive pseudo-label training, EEG-Conformer members, posterior aggregation, and Hungarian assignment decoding under block quotas. fNIRS contributes negligibly (+0.0025), indicating that frequency-diverse temporal EEG modeling and protocol-matched structured inference are key.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Imagined handwriting decoding across unseen participants is challenging because scalp EEG is noisy and internally generated stroke sequences vary across individuals. The Multimodal BCI Grand Challenge provides synchronized EEG and fNIRS data for four-class subject-independent classification, motivating the need for a system that can robustly generalize across participants without test-set adaptation.

Method: FRED models imagined handwriting as a multi-second motor sequence and trains a compact multi-scale temporal network on three complementary EEG frequency views. With three seeds per view, cross-band members produce less-correlated errors than same-band replicas, forming a nine-member ensemble. The pipeline further includes transductive pseudo-label training, three EEG-Conformer members, posterior aggregation, and a paradigm-aware decoder. Final predictions use Hungarian assignment under the known block quota (12 trials per block, 3 per class).

Result: The nine-member ensemble achieves 0.8076/0.7242/0.7492 on public/private/overall test partitions without adaptation. The complete system reaches 0.8498/0.7718/0.7952, ranking fourth on the private split. fNIRS-only decoding is at chance (0.2511 overall), and adding fNIRS to EEG changes accuracy by only +0.0025. Block-constrained decoding (0.7952) outperforms session-constrained (0.7758) and independent decoding (0.7600).

Conclusion: Frequency-diverse temporal EEG modeling and protocol-matched structured inference (block-constrained Hungarian assignment) are the principal sources of performance in this sparse-montage EEG–fNIRS setting. fNIRS contributes little, suggesting that EEG alone with proper temporal modeling and decoding constraints suffices for accurate imagined handwriting classification.

Abstract: Imagined handwriting offers a temporally rich paradigm for non-invasive neural decoding, yet reliable recognition across unseen participants remains difficult because scalp EEG is noisy and internally generated stroke sequences vary across individuals. The Multimodal Brain-Computer Interface Grand Challenge provides synchronized EEG and fNIRS for four-class subject-independent handwriting-trajectory classification. We propose FRED, a task-adapted system that models imagined handwriting as a multi-second motor sequence and trains a compact multi-scale temporal network on three complementary EEG frequency views. With three seeds per view, cross-band members produce substantially less-correlated errors than same-band replicas, yielding a clean nine-member ensemble accuracy of 0.8076/0.7242/0.7492 on the public/private/overall test partitions without test-set adaptation or output constraints. The submitted pipeline further incorporates transductive pseudo-label training, three EEG-Conformer members, posterior aggregation, and a paradigm-aware decoder. Because every 12-trial randomization block contains three instances of each class, the final predictions are obtained by Hungarian assignment under the known block quota. On one fixed posterior pool, independent, session-constrained, and block-constrained decoding achieve 0.7600, 0.7758, and 0.7952 overall accuracy, respectively. The complete system reaches 0.8498/0.7718/0.7952, ranking fourth on the private split. A modality audit finds fNIRS-only decoding at chance (0.2511 overall), while adding fNIRS to EEG changes accuracy by only +0.0025. These results identify frequency-diverse temporal EEG modeling and protocol-matched structured inference as the principal sources of performance in this sparse-montage EEG--fNIRS setting. The source code is available at https://github.com/XiuFan719/EEG-fNIRS-fuse-method-for-MM-challenge.

</details>


### [46] [EditFlow3D: Automated Local Editing of 3D Assets with Trajectory Preservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03179)
*Rui Nie,Chuang Wang,Haitao Zhou,Jiahe Song,Buyu Li,Sheng Wang,Qian Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EditFlow3D是一个无需训练的3D局部编辑框架，利用VLM驱动的流程自动构建视觉引导图像和精炼的3D编辑掩码，通过掩码引导的差分流和逐步轨迹保持，在预训练3D生成模型的原始表示空间中进行局部编辑，同时引入EditFlow-Bench作为补充基准，在更广泛的编辑类别上实现更准确的编辑和非目标区域保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D局部编辑方法缺乏简单准确的3D掩码获取方式，且难以在忠实保持非目标区域结构和外观的同时实现期望的编辑效果。

Method: 提出EditFlow3D框架：1) VLM驱动的工作流自动解析编辑意图并构建视觉引导图像和精炼3D编辑掩码；2) 掩码引导的差分流聚焦目标区域编辑；3) 逐步轨迹保持维持非目标区域与源资产的一致性，无需直接替换中间特征。

Result: 在Edit3D-Bench和新提出的EditFlow-Bench上的定量结果、定性比较和用户研究表明，EditFlow3D在目标区域编辑准确性和非目标区域保持方面优于现有3D编辑方法。

Conclusion: EditFlow3D通过无需训练的方式实现了更准确的局部3D编辑，同时更好地保持了非目标区域的结构和外观。

Abstract: Controllable local editing of 3D assets requires precise target localization and appropriate visual guidance. However, existing methods lack a simple yet accurate way to obtain 3D masks and struggle to achieve the desired edit while faithfully preserving the structure and appearance of non-target regions. To address these challenges, we present EditFlow3D, a training-free framework for local 3D editing. Given a source asset and an edit instruction, a VLM-driven workflow interprets the editing intent and automatically constructs a visual guidance image and a refined 3D editing mask, enabling localized editing in the native representation space of a pretrained 3D generative model. Specifically, mask-guided differential flow focuses the edit on the target region, while step-wise trajectory preservation maintains consistency between non-target regions and the source asset without directly replacing intermediate features. Since the existing Edit3D-Bench covers only a limited range of local editing categories, we further introduce EditFlow-Bench as a complementary benchmark encompassing a broader variety of structural and appearance edits, and evaluate EditFlow3D on both benchmarks. Quantitative results, qualitative comparisons, and a user study demonstrate that EditFlow3D achieves more accurate target-region editing and better preserves non-target regions than existing 3D editing methods.

</details>

### [47] [TabletCraft: Bridging a 4,000-Year Cultural Gap with Bidirectional Akkadian NMT and Cuneiform Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02609)
*Zhaohui Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍TabletCraft，首个支持双向交互的楔形文字开源系统，实现阿卡德语与英语互译及楔形文字渲染，填补了非专业用户无法主动创作内容的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NLP工具仅支持从阿卡德语到英语的单向翻译，面向学者；非专业用户无法用楔形文字创作新内容，只能被动消费古代文化，存在4000年的文化鸿沟。

Method: 系统集成了基于ByT5的翻译模型（训练于116K双向样本）、含14,240个映射（覆盖95.3%）的楔形文字符号转换器以及视觉化的泥板渲染器，封装为可通过pip安装的工具包，提供命令行和Web演示。

Result: 在保留的Akkademia验证集（2,812样本）上，阿卡德语→英语BLEU为49.1，英语→阿卡德语BLEU为48.5，这是反向翻译方向的首个公开定量结果。

Conclusion: TabletCraft首次实现了楔形文字的双向读写，使非专业用户能够主动参与美索不达米亚文化的创作与交流，显著降低了古代文化的参与门槛。

Abstract: Half a million cuneiform clay tablets survive in museums worldwide, yet modern users can neither read nor write in the world's oldest writing system, leaving a 4,000-year cultural barrier that existing NLP tools have only partially addressed.
  Prior work enables one-way, scholar-oriented translation from Akkadian to English, but offers no path in the reverse direction: non-specialist users cannot compose new content in cuneiform, and therefore remain passive consumers of ancient culture rather than active participants.
  We present TabletCraft, the first open-source system that enables bidirectional interaction with Mesopotamian writing. Users can read ancient tablets (Akkadian to English) and compose new messages as cuneiform clay tablets (English to Akkadian to cuneiform to rendered tablet).
  The system integrates a ByT5-based translation model trained on 116K bidirectional samples, a cuneiform sign converter with 14,240 mappings (95.3% coverage), and a visual tablet renderer, packaged as a pip-installable toolkit with CLI and web demo.
  On the held-out Akkademia validation split (2,812 samples), we report 49.1 BLEU for Akkadian-to-English and 48.5 BLEU for English-to-Akkadian, the first published quantitative result in the reverse direction.

</details>


### [48] [BBOWP-Bench: Evaluating LLMs on Black-Box Optimization Word Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02612)
*Yutaro Yamada,Kei Hiroshima,Nozomu Yoshinari,Kento Uchida,Shinichi Shirakawa*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了黑箱优化文字问题（BBOWP）这一新问题设置，要求系统从自然语言描述中推断出搜索空间和优化算法。为了支持该领域的研究，作者建立了BBOWP-Bench基准套件，包含数据集和评估框架。通过对当前LLM的评估发现，LLM能够根据给定的评估预算选择合适的算法，但在搜索空间设计方面存在困难，特别是在识别重要变量和平衡变量范围方面表现不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 优化问题的形式化定义对最终解的质量有很大影响，但好的形式化通常需要丰富的专业知识。现有研究尝试从自然语言描述中自动推导优化问题，但现有基准仅适用于目标函数和约束条件可显式表示为数学表达式的场景。许多实际问题本质上是黑箱优化问题，只能观测到目标值而无法获知函数形式。在黑箱优化中，搜索空间设计（问题形式化的一部分）和优化算法的选择对于问题求解至关重要。利用大语言模型自动化这些过程是一个重要挑战。

Method: 本文定义了黑箱优化文字问题（BBOWP）这一新问题设置，并构建了BBOWP-Bench基准套件。该基准包含：自然语言问题描述、可执行的评估环境、以及人工设计的基线形式化方案。评估框架同时衡量搜索空间设计和算法选择的质量。作者使用此基准对当前LLM进行了首次评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，当前LLM能够根据给定的评估预算选择合适的优化算法，但在搜索空间设计方面存在困难，特别是当问题描述信息不够充分或搜索空间高度依赖具体问题时，LLM在识别重要变量和平衡变量范围方面表现不佳。

Conclusion: 本文为自动化解黑箱优化问题中的搜索空间设计和算法选择提供了新的问题设置和基准，揭示了LLM在此任务上的能力与局限性，为后续研究提供了基础。代码和数据集已开源。

Abstract: Formulating an optimization problem strongly affects the quality of the final solution, yet good formulations usually require substantial expertise. Recent studies have therefore examined how to automatically derive optimization problems from natural-language descriptions, but existing benchmarks focus on settings where objectives and constraints can be written explicitly as mathematical expressions. Many practically important problems are naturally treated as black-box optimization (BBO) problems, in which only objective values are observable, and the functional form is unavailable. In BBO, the search space design, a part of the problem formulation, and the selection of the optimization algorithm are crucial for problem-solving. Automating these processes with large language models (LLMs) is a significant challenge. This paper introduces Black-Box Optimization Word Problems (BBOWP), a novel problem setting in which a system must infer both a search space and an optimization algorithm from a natural-language description of a black-box optimization task. To support research on this setting, we establish the BBOWP Benchmark Suite (BBOWP-Bench), a dataset and evaluation framework for BBOWP. Each instance combines a natural-language problem description, an executable evaluation environment, and a human-designed baseline formulation, allowing evaluation of both search-space design and algorithm selection. Using this benchmark, we provide the first evaluation of LLMs and show that current LLMs are capable of selecting suitable algorithms based on the given evaluation budget. However, they sometimes struggle with search space design, particularly in identifying important variables and balancing their ranges when the problem description is less informative or the search space is highly problem-specific. Our code and dataset are available at https://github.com/shiralab/bbowp-bench.

</details>


### [49] [MemArena: An Ego-Centric Benchmark for On-Device Agentic Personal Memory Assistants at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02613)
*Jiadong Zhang,Xiaosong Ma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 MemArena，一个基于 MASim 模拟器构建的单世界对话基准，用于评估边缘部署的个人记忆助手。该基准包含 50 个智能体在 15 天内的交互（1030 万对话文本令牌，每个智能体每天 24.1K 文本令牌），并联合生成六维评估指标（回忆、推理、可信度）。实验评估了五种开放权重阅读器和多种记忆后端，发现记忆后端的选择对内容准确性影响显著（如 Qwen3-0.6B 上 Memobase 到 MemSearch 提升 +32.5/+19.2 个百分点，超过阅读器扩展的收益），权限感知访问普遍失败，而搜索延迟仅在极小阅读器上明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的记忆基准通常未能充分测试高密度交互活动、自我中心视角和连贯的多会话世界组合场景，而边缘部署的个人记忆助手需要处理私人人际对话并依赖开放权重模型在设备上运行，因此亟需一个能填补这些空白的基准。

Method: 使用 MASim 代理模拟器构建 MemArena 单世界对话基准，模拟 50 个智能体在 15 天内的交互，生成 1030 万对话令牌和每日每智能体 24.1K 文本令牌的自我观察数据。基于交互历史联合生成六个评估维度（回忆、推理、可信度）的 ground truth。评估五种开放权重阅读器（Vanilla context、BM25-RAG、Oracle retrieval、Memobase、MemSearch）作为记忆后端。

Result: (1) 记忆后端的选择比阅读器扩展对内容准确性的影响更大：在 Qwen3-0.6B 上，从 Memobase 切换到 MemSearch 带来 +32.5/+19.2 个百分点的提升，超过 MemSearch 阅读器扩展的收益（+10.6/+6.8 个百分点）。(2) 权限感知访问普遍失败：Oracle 检索严重泄露信息，其他后端过于保守。(3) 搜索延迟仅在极小阅读器上成为问题：在 Spark GB10 边缘节点上，记忆搜索增加 87/7/48 毫秒（BM25-RAG/Memobase/MemSearch），对大多数阅读器-后端组合的 TTFT 影响较小。

Conclusion: MemArena 提供了一个更贴近实际边缘部署场景的基准，揭示了记忆后端优化比扩大模型规模更关键，当前权限感知机制存在严重缺陷，以及搜索延迟在大多数情况下并非瓶颈。代码、MASim 模拟器和 MemArena-L 基准将在接收后开源。

Abstract: Edge-deployed personal memory assistants must handle private interpersonal conversations on-device with open-weight models. Yet, existing memory benchmarks often under-test the combination of activity-dense interaction, ego-centric perspective, and coherent multi-session worlds. MemArena fills these gaps with a single-world conversational benchmark built with its MASim agent simulator, for 50 agents over 15 days (10.3M dialog-text tokens, 24.1K text-only ego-observed tokens/agent/day). With the interaction history, it co-generates ground truth over six recall, reasoning, and trustworthiness evaluation dimensions. We evaluate five open-weight readers with Vanilla context, BM25-RAG, Oracle retrieval, Memobase, and MemSearch as memory backends. Three results stand out: (1) Memory-backend choice matters more for content accuracy: At Qwen3-0.6B, Memobase-to-MemSearch gains +32.5/+19.2 pp, exceeding MemSearch reader scaling (+10.6/+6.8 pp). (2) Permission-aware access fails universally, with Oracle leaking heavily and other backends too timid to disclose. (3) Search latency bites only at very small reader: on a Spark GB10 edge node, memory-search adds a moderate and fixed 87/7/48 ms (BM25-RAG/Memobase/MemSearch) that composes a small part of TTFT for most reader-backend combinations. Code, the MASim simulator, and the MemArena-L benchmark will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [50] [OncoTriad-QA: A Patient-Level Radiology-Pathology-Genomics Benchmark for Pan-Cancer Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02615)
*Ahnaf Munir,Dannong Wang,Michael W. McDonald,Mubarak Shah,Pegah Khosravi,Yu Tian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了OncoTriad-QA基准测试，一个患者级别的放射学-病理学-基因组学综合癌症问答数据集，包含86.1k问题覆盖32种癌症类型9,281个TCGA病例；同时提出OncoVLM多模态模型，通过可学习的投影器将放射学、病理学、DNA甲基化和RNA-seq证据映射到LLM接口。实验表明现有模型在综合癌症QA上表现有限，而OncoVLM在微调后比MedGemma-4B平均高出10.7个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 癌症诊断需要整合放射学、病理学、基因组学和临床元数据等多种证据，但现有医学大语言模型和视觉语言模型基准测试仅关注单一模态或狭窄的图像-文本任务，缺乏多证据流整合的患者级肿瘤评估基准。

Method: 构建了OncoTriad-QA基准，包含来自TCGA的9,281名患者、32种癌症类型的86.1k语义问题，对齐CT/MRI放射学、全切片组织病理学、体细胞突变、拷贝数变异、DNA甲基化、bulk RNA-seq和临床元数据。通过基于源数据的LLM辅助流水线构建病例特异性标注，包括诊断报告、分子图谱和模态证据，并经过自动一致性检查和临床医生审查。同时提出OncoVLM多模态模型，使用可学习投影器将各模态原生证据映射到LLM接口。

Result: 现有的通用和医学LLM在综合癌症QA上表现有限，尤其是需要整合影像学发现、肿瘤形态学和分子证据的问题。OncoVLM在OncoTriad-QA上微调后，在选择题和开放题设置下（放射学、病理学或全部可用模态）始终优于MedGemma-4B，平均提升10.7个（MCQ准确率和BERTScore-F1）。

Conclusion: OncoTriad-QA基准测试对于训练和评估整合式癌症问答模型具有重要价值，能够有效衡量模型在放射学、病理学和基因组学等多模态证据融合方面的能力。

Abstract: Cancer diagnosis and characterization require integrating complementary evidence from radiology, pathology, genomics, and clinical metadata. However, most medical large language model (LLM) and vision-language model (VLM) benchmarks focus on isolated modalities or narrow image-text tasks, leaving patient-level oncology assessment across multiple evidence streams largely untested. We introduce OncoTriad-QA, a patient-level radiology-pathology-genomics benchmark for pan-cancer question answering. OncoTriad-QA contains 86.1k semantic questions across 9,281 TCGA patient cases from 32 cancer cohorts, aligning CT/MRI radiology, whole-slide histopathology, somatic mutations, copy-number alterations, DNA methylation, bulk RNA-seq, and clinical metadata. Case-specific annotations are constructed through a source-grounded LLM-assisted pipeline using curated labels, diagnostic reports, molecular profiles, and modality-derived evidence as primary sources of truth, with automated consistency checks and clinician review. We also introduce OncoVLM, a reference multimodal model that maps modality-native radiology, pathology, DNA methylation, and RNA-seq evidence into an LLM interface through learned projectors. Experiments show that existing general-purpose and medical LLMs remain limited on comprehensive pan-cancer QA, especially when questions require integrating imaging findings, tumor morphology, and molecular evidence. After fine-tuning on OncoTriad-QA, OncoVLM exceeds MedGemma-4B by an average of 10.7 points when using MCQ accuracy and BERTScore-F1, with consistent gains across multiple-choice and open-ended questions under radiology-only, pathology-only, and all-available settings. These results demonstrate the benchmark's value for training and evaluating models for integrated cancer question answering.

</details>


### [51] [Evaluating OpenAI's Privacy Filter: Cross-Lingual, Cross-Domain PII Detection Across 42 Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02616)
*Rohith Uppala*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次独立系统评估了OpenAI的隐私过滤器（OPF），一个15亿参数的双向PII检测器，在42个合成基准测试（22种语言、5个领域）上的表现。零样本下，OPF在AI4Privacy上F1=0.855，在SPY医疗上0.464，优于Presidio和XLM-RoBERTa；但在多语言NER上，XLM-RoBERTa在所有13种印度语和非拉丁语系上领先。GPT-4o在医疗、法律和金融PII上领先，OPF在结构化合成PII和客户支持上领先。OPF在叙事散文嵌入PII时性能急剧下降，对非拉丁语系表现崩溃。错误分析显示OPF在结构规则的PII类型上最强，在文化变异性大的类型上最弱，整体精度在0.31-0.86之间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PII检测方法（如Presidio、XLM-RoBERTa）在跨语言、跨领域的泛化能力不足，且缺乏对OpenAI隐私过滤器（OPF）这一商业PII检测器的独立、系统评估。作者旨在填补这一空白，通过构建多语言、多领域的合成基准，全面衡量OPF的零样本性能，并与开源模型和GPT-4o进行对比。

Method: 构建42个合成基准测试，覆盖22种语言和5个领域（医疗、法律、金融、客户支持、结构化合成PII）。采用零样本评估方式，将OPF（1.5B参数双向PII检测器）与Presidio、XLM-RoBERTa、GPT-4o等模型在PII标注基准和多语言NER基准上进行对比。通过错误分析（精度、召回率、F1）按PII类型、语言、领域进行细粒度性能分解。

Result: OPF零样本F1在AI4Privacy上0.855、SPY医疗上0.464，优于Presidio和XLM-RoBERTa。但XLM-RoBERTa在13种印度语和非拉丁语系NER上领先。GPT-4o在医疗、法律、金融PII上领先（SPY:0.643 avg, Gretel:0.527），OPF在结构化合成PII（0.71 avg）和客户支持（0.60）上领先。OPF在叙事散文嵌入PII时F1降至0.04-0.57，非拉丁语系（阿拉伯语0.04、西里尔语0.03）崩溃。结构规则PII（邮箱0.78、电话0.76）表现好，文化变异性PII（人名0.40、地址0.49）表现差。全局精度0.31-0.86。

Conclusion: OPF在结构化、规则明确的PII检测上表现优异，尤其在客户支持和合成数据场景中，但严重缺乏对叙事文本和非拉丁语系的泛化能力，精度偏低且召回偏置明显。当前OPF更适合作为结构化PII检测工具，而非通用隐私保护方案。未来需改进其跨语言、跨文体泛化能力，并平衡精度-召回率。

Abstract: We present the first independent, systematic evaluation of OpenAI's Privacy Filter (OPF), a 1.5B-parameter bidirectional PII detector, across 42 synthetic benchmarks spanning 22 languages and 5 domains. Zero-shot, OPF achieves F1=0.855 on AI4Privacy and 0.464 on SPY medical, outperforming Presidio (0.431, 0.273) and XLM-RoBERTa (0.269, 0.111) on PII-annotated benchmarks; on multilingual NER, XLM-RoBERTa leads OPF on all 13 Indic and non-Latin languages. GPT-4o leads on medical, legal, and financial PII (SPY: 0.643 avg, Gretel: 0.527), while OPF leads on structured synthetic PII (0.71 avg) and customer support (0.60). OPF degrades sharply when PII is embedded in narrative prose: F1=0.04--0.57 on NER benchmarks and collapse for non-Latin scripts (Arabic: 0.04, Cyrillic: 0.03). Error analysis shows OPF is strongest on structurally regular PII types (email: 0.78, phone: 0.76) and weakest on culturally variable ones (person: 0.40, address: 0.49), and is recall-biased on customer-support and medical/legal PII (P=0.31--0.54, R=0.70--0.85); global precision spans 0.31--0.86 across all domains.

</details>


### [52] [Preferred, Not Safer: Pairwise Preference Is a Poor Proxy for Clinical Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02617)
*Fay Elhassan,David Sasu,Alexandra Kulinkina,Lars Henning Klein,Mary-Anne Hartley*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文通过MOOVE平台收集736+名临床医生对13个LLM的26,804组成对偏好判断，发现临床医生的成对偏好并不能可靠反映LLM的临床安全性。偏好排名高的模型在无害性和准确性等安全关键维度上仍可能出现大量临床意义上的失败，且失败分布具有专科特异性，造成领域特定的“禁区”。研究进一步分析了提示长度、拒绝/升级行为以及表面特征与安全关键特征对偏好的相对贡献，并引入了一种结合偏好与评分标准的临床调整偏好排名，以产生更安全导向的排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM评估常依赖人类偏好作为代理指标，但临床环境下安全关键性能与偏好的关系尚不明确。本研究旨在验证临床医生成对偏好能否可靠反映LLM的临床安全性，并揭示偏好与安全之间的潜在偏差。

Method: 利用MOOVE平台收集临床医生对13个LLM输出的成对偏好判断（26,804对）及多维度评分标准评估（离散-2到+2量表）。通过统计分析和特征分解，比较偏好排名与安全关键维度（如无害性、准确性）的表现，并引入Bradley-Terry模型与评分标准反馈结合的临床调整偏好排名。

Result: 临床医生偏好是安全关键性能的较差代理指标；偏好排名高的模型在无害性和准确性上仍可能出现大量≤-1的临床失败；失败分布存在专科特异性；大量偏好投票不携带正面安全信号；表面特征对偏好变化的解释力略高于安全关键特征差异。

Conclusion: 评估实践中应将偏好与安全性分离，直接报告安全关键失败率，并在对LLM进行临床决策排名时纳入临床调整机制。

Abstract: We evaluate whether clinician pairwise preferences provide a reliable signal of clinical safety in large language model (LLM) evaluation using expert feedback from MOOVE (Massive Open Online Validation and Evaluation), a clinician-led platform collecting blinded pairwise preferences alongside multi-criterion rubric ratings. Clinicians assign scores on a discrete $[-2, +2]$ scale, where negative values indicate clinically unsafe or misleading content. Using 26{,}804 pairwise judgments across outputs from 13 LLMs, contributed by more than 736 clinicians across 28+ countries, we find that clinician preference is a poor proxy for safety-critical performance. Models ranking highly under pairwise preference can still exhibit substantial rates of clinically meaningful failures ($\leq -1$) on dimensions such as \emph{Harmlessness} and \emph{Accuracy}. These failures are unevenly distributed across specialties, creating domain-specific ``no-go zones'' not visible in aggregate rankings or single-number leaderboards. We further analyze contributing factors including prompt length, refusal and escalation behavior, and the relative contributions of safety-critical versus surface-level features. A substantial fraction of preference votes carry no positive safety signal, while feature decomposition shows that surface-level characteristics explain slightly more preference variation than safety-critical rubric differences. Finally, we introduce a clinically adjusted preference ranking combining pairwise preference with rubric-derived feedback, producing a more safety-aware ordering than raw Bradley--Terry strength alone. Our findings support evaluation practices that separate preference from safety, report safety-critical failure rates directly, and incorporate clinically grounded adjustments when ranking LLMs for clinical decision making.

</details>


### [53] [JudgeArena: A Unified Framework for Reproducible LLM-Judge Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02620)
*Erlis Lushtaku,Bora Kargi,Ali Elganzory,Fabio Ferreira,Alejandro R. Salamanca,Julia Kreutzer,David Salinas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: JudgeArena 是一个开源框架，统一了多个 LLM-as-a-judge 基准测试（AlpacaEval、Arena-Hard、MT-Bench、m-Arena-Hard），支持可替换的评判模型、全面的元数据日志，并提供开源评判模型的调优配置，能够以低成本模拟 LMArena Elo 评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 LLM-as-a-judge 评估生态碎片化严重：大多数基准测试使用独立的代码库、硬编码特定的闭源评判模型，并只支持单一的评估协议。这种碎片化使得系统性地研究设计选择（基准测试、评判模型、提示词、推理后端）对模型质量结论的影响变得困难，同时也缺乏透明度和可复现性。

Method: JudgeArena 通过统一多个主流 LLM 评判基准测试到单一接口来解决碎片化问题，支持通过 vLLM、llama.cpp 或 OpenRouter 接入任意模型作为候选模型或评判模型。它包含调优的评判配置使开源模型匹配或超越闭源评判模型的性能，并通过结合人工标注与 LLM 评判评估来模拟 LMArena Elo 分数。

Result: JudgeArena 提供了调优后的开源评判模型配置，在英语和多语言人工偏好数据集上验证，性能匹配或超越闭源评判模型。同时，它能够以高准确度模拟 LMArena Elo 评分，为大规模人工标注提供了实用、开放且低成本的替代方案。

Conclusion: JudgeArena 通过统一接口、支持可替换的评判模型、增强透明度和可复现性，有效缓解了 LLM 评估生态的碎片化问题。它为系统研究设计选择的影响提供了基础设施，并降低了对闭源模型的依赖，推动了更开放、低成本的模型评估实践。

Abstract: LLM-as-a-judge evaluation has become a dominant paradigm for ranking language models, yet the ecosystem remains fragmented: most benchmarks ship their own code base, hardcode a specific closed-model judge, and support a single evaluation protocol. This fragmentation makes it difficult to study how design choices--the benchmark, the judge model, the prompt, the inference backend--affect the conclusions we draw about model quality. We introduce JudgeArena, an open-source framework that unifies major LLM-judge benchmarks (AlpacaEval, Arena-Hard, MT-Bench, and m-Arena-Hard) under a single interface with swappable judges and comprehensive metadata logging for increased transparency in reporting and reproducibility. It enables systematic studies of judge choices, as any model accessible via vLLM, llama.cpp, or OpenRouter can serve as both candidate and judge. Furthermore, JudgeArena ships with tuned judge configurations for open models that match or outperform closed-model judges, validated on human preference datasets in both English and multilingual settings, reducing the reliance on opaque closed models. Finally, by combining existing human annotations with LLM-judge evaluations of a target model, JudgeArena can simulate LMArena Elo scores with high accuracy offering a practical, open, and low-cost alternative to large-scale human annotation campaigns.

</details>


### [54] [Knowing the Form, Not the Function: Automatically Auditing Answer--Authority Decoupling in Legal Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02621)
*Hsien-Jyh Liao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 当前法律基准测试仅评分最终答案的正确性，忽略了模型是否引用正确的法律条文（权威依据）。本文通过中国台湾地区律师资格考试题目、刑事法律子集及中国民法初步扩展实验，发现大语言模型在未要求引用法条时仍会自发标注权威依据，但答案正确性与权威依据引用之间存在双向分离：部分答案正确但未引用权威法条，部分答案错误却引用了正确法条。进一步实验表明答案与引用行为可在输出层面独立变化。因此，作者提出联合评估答案正确性与权威依据引用的评分方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前法律领域基准测试仅关注最终答案的正确性，即使模型输出了法律权威依据（如具体法条）也不纳入评分。这无法反映模型是否真正基于正确法律依据推理，可能导致“答案正确但法条错误”或“答案错误但法条正确”的情况被掩盖。因此，需要检验答案正确性能否作为权威依据引用的代理指标，并探索是否需要联合评估。

Method: 使用238道中国台湾地区律师资格考试题目（每道题有已验证的管辖法条），在普通推理提示（不要求引用法条）下测试四个大语言模型。自动联合审计答案正确性与权威依据引用情况。另外设计了一个法条检索探针实验和一个允许引用回避的干预实验，观察答案与引用行为能否分离。并初步扩展到中国民法领域进行跨法域验证。

Result: 在法律（刑事法）子集中，24.0%–42.4%的有效回答答案正确但未引用黄金法条（权威依据缺失），15.2%–21.7%的回答答案错误但引用了正确法条。法条检索探针和引用回避干预实验均表明答案正确性与引用行为可以在输出层面独立变化。这种分离在非对抗性、非一致性诱导提示下自然产生。

Conclusion: 仅基于答案正确性的评分会将在自然情况下发生的权威依据缺失视为完全正确的基准测试通过，未能全面评估模型的法律推理质量。由于法条权威依据在结构上可提取且外部可验证，这种失败可以自动测量。因此，对于基于法条的法律基准测试，应联合评估答案正确性与权威依据引用，以更准确地反映模型能力。

Abstract: Legal benchmarks typically score final answers even when models also state legal authority. We test whether answer correctness can serve as a proxy for authority grounding. Under ordinary reasoning prompts that did not request statutory citations, four LLMs spontaneously produced authority markers across 238 Taiwan bar-examination items. Because each item has a verified governing provision, we automatically audit answer correctness and authority grounding jointly. The two dimensions dissociate in both directions. In criminal law, 24.0--42.4\% of valid responses were answer-correct but missed the gold authority, while 15.2--21.7\% were answer-incorrect but cited it. A separate statutory-retrieval probe and a permissive citation-abstention intervention further show that answer and citation behavior can move separately at the output level. Because this mismatch arises without adversarial or inconsistency-inducing prompting, answer-only scoring treats naturally occurring gold-authority misses as complete benchmark successes. Because statutory authority is structurally extractable and externally verifiable, the failure can be measured automatically. A preliminary PRC civil-law extension also observes citation-unrequested authority marking, motivating a full cross-jurisdictional joint audit. We therefore propose joint answer--authority evaluation for statute-grounded legal benchmarks.

</details>


### [55] [Speculative Correction: Draft-then-Refine Decoding for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02625)
*Brian K Chen,Chong Wu,Kenji Kawaguchi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种即插即用的扩散语言模型推理范式：首先生成完整草稿，再利用双向扩散对完整响应进行全局精炼。实验表明，同模型草稿-精炼（Flash-Flash）可提升模型自身块自回归输出的质量与速度，而小模型草稿加大模型精炼（Mini-Flash）则实现了无需训练的高效加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型虽具备双向修正能力，但标准解码过程通常迫使其以从左到右的块自回归方式生成文本，未能充分利用双向扩散的潜力。作者希望探索一种简单通用的推理模式，以激活DLM的双向精炼优势。

Method: 提出两阶段推理范式：第一阶段用模型（或小模型）一次性生成完整草稿；第二阶段利用扩散模型对整个响应进行双向精炼。具体实现两种配置：(1) Flash-Flash：同一Flash模型同时担任起草者和精炼者；(2) Mini-Flash：由更小的Mini模型起草，Flash模型进行精炼（投机修正）。

Result: Flash-Flash在GSM8K-384上准确率从0.848提升至0.899，同时速度比块自回归基线快1.20倍；MBPP-384从0.545提升至0.693。因果消融表明完整草稿提供了有用的初始化，全局精炼在GSM8K上增益明显，局部精炼在MBPP和MATH上捕获大部分增益。Mini-Flash在MATH-384上达到0.294 vs Flash的0.300，但速度快2.17倍。结果支持帕累托前沿解释而非异构级联统一匹配Flash质量。

Conclusion: 同模型草稿-精炼证明双向精炼是扩散语言模型的一种有用的解码原语，投机修正则展示了一条无需训练即可实现快速生成的有效路径。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (DLMs) can revise tokens bidirectionally, but standard decoding procedures often adapt them to left-to-right generation by producing text block by block. We study a simple plug-and-play inference pattern: first generate a complete draft, then refine the full response using bidirectional diffusion. Using LLaDA2.1-Flash and LLaDA2.1-Mini, we evaluate two configurations. In Flash-Flash, the same Flash model serves as both drafter and refiner, testing whether an existing model can improve its own block-autoregressive output through global refinement. In Mini-Flash, inspired by speculative decoding, we introduce speculative correction: Mini drafts a full response, and Flash revises it as an editable initialization. Flash-Flash improves GSM8K-384 accuracy from 0.848 to 0.899 while running 1.20 times faster than the selected Flash block-autoregressive baseline, and improves MBPP-384 from 0.545 to 0.693. Latency-window-matched Flash-only controls indicate that these gains persist after targeted tuning of block-autoregressive decoding. Causal ablations indicate that completed drafts provide useful initializations: refinement from a fully masked span performs poorly, full global refinement provides a clear additional gain on GSM8K, and local refinement captures much of the gain on MBPP and MATH. Mini-Flash provides useful quality-latency trade-offs, including MATH-384 performance of 0.294 versus 0.300 for Flash while running 2.17 times faster. These results support a Pareto-frontier interpretation rather than the claim that the heterogeneous cascade uniformly matches Flash quality. Overall, same-model draft-and-refine provides evidence that bidirectional refinement is a useful decoding primitive for DLMs, while speculative correction demonstrates a training-free route to fast DLM generation.

</details>


### [56] [Stuck on "A": Diagnosing and Repairing Interface Injury in Attention-to-KDA Linearization of a 0.6B Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02689)
*Ronglong Bao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 将 Qwen3-0.6B-Base 的 28 个全注意力层中的 21 个转换为 KDA 线性注意力层（在单张消费级 GPU 上），通过隐状态对齐和端到端 KL 蒸馏使困惑度接近教师模型，但多项选择准确率仍接近随机（C-Eval 上 25-29% vs 教师 50.6%）。使用四排列诊断法旋转选项标签，发现模型 81% 预测 "A"、106/161 题在所有旋转下保持同一标签，表明模型追随选项标签而非内容——即标准蒸馏指标无法发现的"界面损伤"。1000 步格式定向的仅完成阶段 KL 蒸馏修复了该损伤（C-Eval +12.48，标签粘性减半），随后的人格 SFT 和一轮在线 DPO 在噪声范围内保持基准分数。公开代码、权重、配方和完整审计追踪，并提炼了在低预算下实现收敛的工程经验（包括 FP32 主权重模式下 bf16 优化器更新被静默吞噬的失败模式）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在全注意力层转换为线性注意力层的过程中，模型究竟丢失了什么能力，以及如何在极低的计算预算下（单张消费级 GPU）通过知识蒸馏恢复性能。特别关注那些被标准困惑度指标掩盖但实际影响下游任务表现的深层次损伤。

Method: 1) 将 Qwen3-0.6B-Base 的 28 个全注意力层中的 21 个替换为 KDA 线性注意力层；2) 使用隐状态对齐（hidden-state alignment）和端到端 KL 散度蒸馏训练学生模型；3) 设计四排列诊断法：在保持问题内容不变的情况下旋转选项顺序（A/B/C/D），检测模型是否固定预测特定标签；4) 进行 1000 步仅针对完成阶段的格式定向 KL 蒸馏（format-targeted completion-only KL）修复界面损伤；5) 添加人格 SFT 和一轮在线 DPO（on-policy DPO）微调。

Result: 蒸馏后学生模型困惑度接近教师，但 C-Eval 多项选择准确率仅 25-29%（随机水平），教师为 50.6%。四排列诊断显示模型 81% 预测 "A"，106/161 题在所有四种排列下预测同一标签，表现出严重的标签粘性（label-stickiness）。格式定向的仅完成阶段 KL 蒸馏将 C-Eval 提升 12.48 分，标签粘性降低约一半。后续 SFT 和 DPO 在噪声范围内保持基准分数。额外发现 FP32 主权重模式下 bf16 优化器更新可能被静默吞噬，导致收敛失败。

Conclusion: 全注意力转线性注意力的主要损伤并非内容理解或语言建模能力，而是模型与任务格式的"接口"——即正确关联选项标签与选项内容的能力。标准蒸馏指标（如困惑度）无法反映这种损伤，但标签排列诊断可以有效检测。通过针对性的格式定向 KL 蒸馏可以高效修复接口损伤。研究还揭示了低预算蒸馏中的关键工程陷阱（如 FP32/bf16 精度问题），为资源受限场景下的模型压缩提供了实用指导。

Abstract: We convert 21 of 28 full-attention layers of Qwen3-0.6B-Base into KDA (Kimi Delta Attention) linear-attention layers on a single consumer-grade GPU budget, and ask a simple question: what exactly does the conversion break? After surgery, hidden-state alignment and end-to-end KL distillation drive the student close to its teacher in perplexity, yet multiple-choice accuracy stays near random chance (25-29% vs. the teacher's 50.6% on C-Eval). Using a four-permutation diagnostic that rotates answer options while holding content fixed, we show the model sticks to option labels (predicting "A" 81% of the time; 106/161 questions keep the same label under all four rotations) rather than following answer content -- an interface injury that standard distillation metrics cannot see. A 1,000-step format-targeted completion-only KL stage repairs the interface (+12.48 points on C-Eval, label-stickiness roughly halved), after which persona SFT and one round of on-policy DPO preserve benchmark scores within noise. We release code, weights, recipes, and the full audit trail, and distill the engineering lessons -- including an FP32-master failure mode in which bf16 optimizer updates are silently swallowed -- that made convergence possible at this budget.

</details>


### [57] [Crayotter: Learning Long-Horizon Video Editing Agents via Group-Relative Preference Backpropagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02694)
*Lecheng Yan,Jianze Lin,Yichong Zhang,Ben Pan,Wenxi Li,Chenyang Lyu,Liting Zhou,Cathal Gurrin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Group-Relative Preference Backpropagation (GRPB)方法，将长程视频编辑中的主观、延迟反馈转化为同任务排序的零和优势，并分配有限信用到语义编辑段，最终训练出9B参数的Crayotter模型，在AgenticVBench上超越多个专有系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程视频编辑代理的最终产品反馈滞后且相互依赖；编辑质量主观、存在多种有效解，跨异构请求缺乏有意义的校准，全局标量目标既模糊又缺乏时间信息。

Method: Group-Relative Preference Backpropagation (GRPB)：将同任务排序转化为零和优势，再作为有界信用分配到语义编辑片段；使用滞后分配器和受保护传输，防止当前判断或不可靠估计直接影响同一rollout组。

Result: 在匹配基线、信用干预、外部基准测试和盲人机评估中，GRPB同时改进了编辑行为和渲染产品；9B Crayotter模型在AgenticVBench上超越多个专有系统。

Conclusion: 支持任务局部偏好约简作为从主观、延迟结果中学习的一种实用方法。

Abstract: Long-horizon video editing agents receive final-product feedback only after many interdependent decisions. Yet editing quality is subjective, admits multiple valid solutions, and is not meaningfully calibrated across heterogeneous requests, making a global scalar objective both ambiguous and temporally uninformative. Our key observation is that fixing the request, materials, and production constraints converts this subjective objective into an ordinal comparison among directly comparable alternatives. We introduce Group-Relative Preference Backpropagation (GRPB), which transforms same-task rankings into zero-sum advantages and redistributes them as bounded credit over semantic editing segments. A lagged allocator and guarded transmission prevent current judgments or unreliable estimates from directly shaping the same rollout group. We manually construct a project-disjoint, horizon-stratified suite of realistic editing tasks for training and controlled evaluation. Across matched baselines, credit interventions, external benchmarking, and blinded human evaluation, GRPB improves both editing behavior and rendered products. The resulting 9B Crayotter model surpasses several proprietary systems on AgenticVBench, supporting task-local preference reduction as a practical approach to learning from subjective, delayed outcomes. Code and all supporting materials are publicly available at https://github.com/idwts/Crayotter.

</details>


### [58] [ARCHead: Activation-Metric Residual Correction for Large Language Model Output Heads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02703)
*Şuayp Talha Kocabay,Talha Rüzgar Akkuş,Kamer Ali Yuksel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ARCHead 是一种针对 LLM 语言建模头（LM-head）的压缩方法，通过结合量化低秩核心、分组 INT4 残差和基于激活度量的低秩校正，在无需密集 BF16 头的情况下实现 3.7-3.9 倍的存储压缩，同时保持极低的困惑度损失（Qwen3-8B-Base 上相对困惑度 1.007），并能无缝替代 AWQ 或 bitsandbytes 等块量化器留下的 BF16 头，仅增加 0.006-0.007 交叉熵和不到 2% 的吞吐量变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的权重量化方法（如 AWQ、bitsandbytes）通常只量化 Transformer 块中的权重，而将最后的语言建模头（LM-head）保留在 BF16/FP16 格式，导致该部分成为存储瓶颈。若简单地对 LM-head 进行量化，会严重扰动词汇-逻辑分布，降低模型性能。因此需要一种专门针对 LM-head 的高效压缩方法。

Method: ARCHead 采用三种组件组合的方式压缩 LM-head：(1) 量化低秩核心（quantized low-rank core），(2) 分组 INT4 残差（group-wise INT4 residuals），(3) 基于激活度量的低秩校正（low-rank correction fitted in an activation-derived metric）。整体不存储任何密集的 BF16 头，而是通过打包压缩表示来替代。

Result: 在 Qwen3-8B-Base 上，ARCHead 仅使用 BF16 头存储的 25.6%，实现 1.007 的相对困惑度；同等存储大小的朴素 INT4 量化相对困惑度为 1.14-1.16。LM-head 存储压缩比为 3.7-3.9 倍。替代 AWQ 或 bitsandbytes 留下的 BF16 头后，交叉熵仅增加 0.006-0.007，吞吐量变化小于 2%。

Conclusion: ARCHead 能够有效压缩 LLM 的 LM-head，填补了块量化方法无法处理输出投影的空白，作为块量化器的补充方案，在几乎不损失性能和效率的前提下大幅减少存储开销。

Abstract: Weight-only quantization substantially reduces the storage of large language model (LLM) transformer blocks, but practical backends often retain the final language-modeling head (LM-head) in BF16 or FP16. Quantizing this projection naively can strongly perturb the vocabulary-logit distribution. We present ARCHead, a packed LM-head compressor that combines a quantized low-rank core, group-wise INT4 residuals, and a low-rank correction fitted in an activation-derived metric. ARCHead stores no dense BF16 head and reduces persistent LM-head storage by 3.7-3.9x. On Qwen3-8B-Base, it uses 25.6% of BF16 head storage while attaining 1.007 relative perplexity; storage-matched naive INT4 yields 1.14-1.16. Replacing the BF16 head left by AWQ or bitsandbytes adds only 0.006-0.007 cross-entropy, with less than 2% throughput change in our measurements. ARCHead therefore complements block quantizers by compressing the large output projection they can leave untouched. Code is available at https://github.com/suayptalha/archead.

</details>


### [59] [Learning a Vector-Symbolic Model for Socio-Cultural Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02807)
*Meera Ray,Swapnika Dulam,Christopher L. Dancy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于ACT-R认知架构的陈述性记忆系统，利用向量符号自编码器表示多层次语义关联，并通过简单的HRR操作区分情景记忆与语义记忆，最终在种族情境化的内隐联想测试（IAT）中进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算认知模型难以充分表征社会文化结构对决策的影响，需要跨越多个语义层次，但建模者不清楚哪些层次最相关。大语言模型和语料库模型虽能表示宽泛语义关联，但未考虑自我表征在记忆中的作用，无法确定文化关联如何塑造决策。因此需要一种能整合多层次语义表征并纳入自我表征的记忆系统。

Method: 在ACT-R架构中设计了一个新的陈述性记忆系统，使用向量符号自编码器（vector-symbolic autoencoder）来学习多层次的语义关联。通过HRR（Holographic Reduced Representations）操作将情景记忆与从文本中提取的语义记忆向量进行不同编码，最终产生用于记忆请求的组块激活值。

Result: 利用ACT-R认知模型在种族情境化的内隐联想测试（IAT）上对该记忆系统进行了测试，验证了其有效性（具体定量结果未在摘要中给出）。

Conclusion: 该工作为计算认知模型更好地表征社会文化结构对决策的影响提供了新途径，通过多层次语义表征和自我表征的整合，有望更准确地模拟文化关联在决策中的作用。

Abstract: How can we better represent the impact of sociocultural structures on decision making in computational cognitive models? Modeling this impact requires traversing multiple levels of semantic representation, however it is not immediately clear to a modeler which levels of representation are most salient to a given situation. Though large language models and cognitively grounded corpus models can represent broad semantic associations through co-occurences, the role of self representations in memory should be accounted for to determine how cultural associations shape decision making. We propose a declarative memory system to be used in the ACT-R cognitive architecture that represents semantic associations at multiple levels via a vector-symbolic autoencoder. We use a simple HRR operation to encode episodic memories differently from semantic memory vectors extracted from text to produce a final chunk activation for a memory request. We use ACT-R cognitive models of a racially contextualized implicit association test (IAT) to test this new declarative memory system.

</details>


### [60] [BODHI: Do LLMs Branch Out and Discover Heterogeneous Inferences?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02867)
*Soumadeep Saha,Krish Sharma,Akshay Chaturvedi,Nicholas Asher*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨了带可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）是否扩展了LLM的推理能力边界，还是仅提升了采样效率。通过迷宫求解实验和从数学推理轨迹中提取基于语义等价性的树结构（BODHI-Trees），发现RLVR虽改善了环境约束遵循和回溯能力，但导致策略熵和语义分支熵显著下降，表明其以牺牲真实探索多样性为代价换取采样效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对RLVR训练后的LLM是否真正扩展了推理能力边界还是仅提升采样效率这一争议，作者希望探究RLVR对测试时探索本质的影响，区分风格变异导致的熵与实际推理分支的熵。

Method: 采用受控迷宫求解实验，并从数学推理轨迹中提取基于语义等价性的BODHI-Trees树结构，以此区分风格变异产生的熵与真正的推理分支熵。

Result: RLVR模型中的策略熵坍塌不仅是句法层面的，还伴随语义分支熵的显著降低。RLVR虽然提升了遵循环境约束和回溯能力，但压缩了续写空间，这可能是采样效率提升的原因，但牺牲了真实的轨迹多样性。

Conclusion: RLVR以牺牲探索多样性为代价提升采样效率，并未真正扩展推理能力边界，而是通过收缩推理路径空间来更高效地采样已有能力范围内的解。

Abstract: Although reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has improved the performance of large language models (LLMs) across a variety of reasoning tasks, there is significant debate as to whether RLVR expands the reasoning capability boundary, or just improves sampling efficiency. In this paper, we investigate the nature of test-time exploration in RLVR-trained LLMs by employing controlled maze-solving experiments and extracting a tree structure from mathematical reasoning traces (BODHI-Trees) based on semantic equivalence. This helps us delineate between entropy arising from stylistic variations and genuine inferential branching. Our findings demonstrate that the policy entropy collapse observed in RLVR models is not merely syntactic, and is accompanied by a significant reduction in semantic branching entropy. While RLVR improves adherence to environmental constraints and backtracking capabilities, it constricts the space of continuations; we provide evidence suggesting that this might be responsible for the sample efficiency gains of RLVR, albeit at the cost of genuine rollout diversity.

</details>


### [61] [FLARE: Few-shot Learning-based Adaptive Reflective Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02919)
*Dhanasekar Sundararaman,Bharat Gandhi,Aashna Garg,Minjie Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FLARE是一个基于少量样本学习的自适应反思引擎，通过结合先进反思机制和少量参考示例来优化指令，在多个基准测试上优于现有优化器GEPA，展示了更高的准确率、数据效率和稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有先进优化器GEPA声称反思指令进化优于传统强化学习和少量样本优化，但本文挑战这一观点，认为少量样本学习的战略优化仍是关键，旨在证明通过少量参考示例和反思机制可以更有效地优化指令。

Method: 提出FLARE框架，利用高级反思机制和少量参考示例（few-shot）来优化指令，在多个基准任务上使用GPT-5系列模型进行评估。

Result: FLARE在所有任务-模型对上一致超越GEPA：在HotPotQA上提升+14.2点（52.2 vs 42.2），工具调用达87.0%（vs 81.0%），GoEmotions微F1提升至52.7%（+15.3），且数据效率高，仅需100个验证示例即可达到峰值性能，同时稳定性更好。

Conclusion: 反思指令虽然强大，但少量样本学习的战略优化仍然是最大化下一代大语言模型潜力的关键前沿。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in complex, compound AI systems where performance hinges on the quality of prompts. Recent state-of-the-art optimizers like GEPA (Genetic-Pareto) have argued that reflective instruction evolution can outperform traditional reinforcement learning and few-shot optimization. In this work, we challenge this shift by introducing FLARE (Few-shot Learning-based Adaptive Reflective Engine), a framework that leverages advanced reflective mechanisms and a small set of few-shot reference examples to optimize instructions. We evaluate our method across a diverse suite of benchmarks -- spanning retrieval-augmented reasoning (HotPotQA, MedQA, 2WikiMultiHopQA), tool calling, and multi-label emotion classification (GoEmotions) -- using the GPT-5 series of models. Our results demonstrate that FLARE consistently outperforms GEPA, winning on every task-model pair: it achieves gains of up to +14.2 points on HotPotQA (52.2 vs. GEPA's 42.2 with GPT-5-Chat), reaches 87.0% on tool calling (vs. 81.0% for GEPA), and lifts GoEmotions micro-F1 to 52.7% (+15.3) with GPT-5.1 on the full 5408-example test split, more than doubling GEPA's +5.7 gain. Beyond raw accuracy, FLARE is also strikingly data-efficient: on GoEmotions it reaches its peak performance using as few as 100 validation examples, while remaining markedly more stable across random seeds than GEPA. Our findings suggest that while reflective instructions are powerful, the strategic optimization of few-shot learning remains a critical frontier for maximizing the potential of next-generation LLMs.

</details>


### [62] [Character Iconicity vs. Arbitrariness: An Arabic NLP Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02935)
*Dorieh Alomari,Irfan Ahmad,Maged S. Al-shaibani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了阿拉伯语点符（dots）对自然语言处理（NLP）性能的必要性。通过对比标准带点阿拉伯语、去点阿拉伯语以及任意但一致的字符重映射（均限制在相同的19个无点rasm集上），发现随机重映射在语言建模、文本分类、序列标注、机器翻译和原始脚本恢复等任务中取得了与原始带点文本相当的性能，同时降低了词汇量、未登录词率、模型大小和训练成本。这表明从NLP角度看，阿拉伯语字符形式-功能关系在很大程度上是任意的，模型更依赖稳定的分布结构而非视觉象似性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
