We introduce a paired, multi-detector evaluation protocol that (i) scores utility only on masked spans, decoupling coverage from utility; (ii) uses equivalence testing (TOST) rather than null-hypothesis significance testing, which is uninformative at our sample size (57k paired spans); and (iii) builds a surrogate-failure typology separating fixable generator defects from intrinsic detector limits. Across 11 detectors, 7 benchmarks, and 7 languages (1,750 documents), recall on masked spans moves from 76.1% to 74.9% -- a change our equivalence test shows is statistically equivalent to zero within a +/-2-point margin (p ~ 3e-9), with detector ranking preserved. The residual loss does not reflect detectors getting worse at PHI: it concentrates in malformed and out-of-distribution surrogates (truncation Chicago -> Illino, salience loss Cedars-Sinai -> Vidant). A redaction floor and an open-source surrogate baseline indicate the effect is a property of well-formed substitution, not of one tool. We release the evaluation subsets, scoring code, and an interactive dashboard at https://custodianai.pages.dev so the protocol can audit any structure-preserving transform.

</details>


### [128] [Diversity is Not Ambiguity: Toward Accurate and Efficient Ambiguity Detection for Open-Domain QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03177)
*Jiwon Lee,Yong-chan Park,Jungin Hong,U Kang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 这篇论文提出了ARCHIVE，一个通过逻辑冲突检测来高效识别开放域问答中歧义查询的框架，并构建了QuireQA基准。实验表明，ARCHIVE在准确性和速度上均显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将答案多样性误认为歧义，导致误判；且对所有查询统一处理，计算效率低下。因此需要更准确高效的歧义检测方法。

Method: ARCHIVE基于逻辑冲突检测歧义：若一个查询的有效答案无法在单一解释下同时为真，则该查询有歧义。它结合了轻量级早退编码器处理表面可检测案例，以及冲突推理模块建模答案间的逻辑关系，并通过不变性目标增强对噪声答案集的鲁棒性。

Result: 在QuireQA基准上，ARCHIVE在F1-amb和F1-unamb上分别提升最多10.4%和21.6%，且速度比最佳竞争者快16倍。

Conclusion: 逻辑冲突是检测歧义的有效标准，结合级联架构和冲突推理能实现准确且高效的歧义检测。

Abstract: How can question answering (QA) systems determine whether a query is ambiguous? Ambiguity detection is essential in open-domain QA, as misclassification leads to answering the wrong interpretation or unnecessary clarification. However, existing methods conflate answer diversity with ambiguity, leading to inaccurate predictions. They also process queries uniformly, resulting in wasteful computation. We propose ARCHIVE (Ambiguity Recognition via Cascaded Hypothesis Inspection and Conflict Verification), an accurate and efficient framework that detects ambiguity via logical conflict: a query is ambiguous when its valid answers cannot all be true under a single interpretation. ARCHIVE combines a lightweight early-exit encoder for surface-detectable cases with a conflict reasoning module that models logical relations among answers, reinforced by an invariance objective for robustness to noisy answer sets. We present QuireQA, a 4,703-query benchmark spanning factoid, non-factoid, and ill-formed queries. Experiments show ARCHIVE outperforms competitors, improving F1-amb by up to 10.4% and F1-unamb by up to 21.6%, while operating 16$\times$ faster than the best competitor.

</details>


### [129] [TumorBoard: Evidence-Grounded Multi-Agent Decision Support for Longitudinal Neuro-Oncology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03190)
*Yantong Liu,Zheyu Zhang,Runpeng Liu,Mu Xitang,Seong-Yoon Shin,Hyun-Ae Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TumorBoard 是一个基于多智能体协作的神经肿瘤学决策支持系统，利用共享病历状态、可审计的声明-证据账本、专科智能体、对抗性批评者和安全调控器，在 360 例隐藏测试集上实现了动作 F1 0.772、证据蕴含 0.914，并显著超越强基线，同时有效降低了不安全建议的发布率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经肿瘤学临床决策需要综合解读序列 MRI、病理、分子标记物、治疗史、体能状态和不断更新的指南，现有系统缺乏对多源证据的结构化协调、时间有效性和可审计性，导致决策支持不完整或不可靠。

Method: 构建多智能体系统 TumorBoard，包含：(1) 共享纵向病例状态作为全局上下文；(2) 可审计的声明-证据账本记录每个原子声明及其来源；(3) 放射学、神经病理、分子诊断、指南和治疗规划五个专科智能体各自生成带有溯源的声明；(4) 对抗性批评者检测矛盾；(5) 安全调控器根据证据充分性和时间有效性决定发布、限定或推迟推荐。

Result: 在 360 例隐藏基准上，TumorBoard 达到动作 F1 0.772、证据蕴含 0.914，比最强的类型化议会基线高出 3.1 个百分点（95% CI 1.6–4.7，校正 p=0.0012），推荐-证据覆盖率达 0.927。在证据删除测试下，系统推迟了 84.2% 的不安全案例，有害推荐仅占 5.8%。安全调控器使有害发布降低 7.8 个百分点，代价为 4.3 个百分点的误推迟。消融实验验证了账本、批评者和调控器各自的作用，证明结构化协调是多智能体优势的来源。

Conclusion: TumorBoard 通过结构化多智能体协调、可审计的声明-证据账本和分级安全控制，显著提升了神经肿瘤学决策支持的准确性和安全性，为高风险临床决策支持系统提供了一种可信、可验证的设计范式。

Abstract: Neuro-oncology decisions require coordinated interpretation of serial MRI, pathology, molecular markers, treatment history, performance status, and evolving guidelines. We present TumorBoard, a multi-agent decision-support system built around a shared longitudinal case state and an auditable claim-evidence ledger. Specialist agents for radiology, neuropathology, molecular diagnosis, guidelines, and therapy planning produce atomic claims with provenance. An adversarial critic exposes contradictions, and a safety governor releases, qualifies, or defers recommendations according to evidence sufficiency and temporal validity. On a 360-case hidden benchmark at a matched token budget, TumorBoard achieved an action F1 of 0.772 and evidence entailment of 0.914. It exceeded the strongest typed-council baseline by 3.1 percentage points (95% CI: 1.6 to 4.7, adjusted p = 0.0012), while recommendation-to-evidence coverage reached 0.927. Under evidence deletion, the system deferred 84.2% of unsafe cases and limited harmful recommendations to 5.8%. The safety governor reduced harmful release by 7.8 percentage points at a false-deferral cost of 4.3 percentage points. Ablation studies of the ledger, critic, and governor produced the predicted failure patterns, establishing structured coordination as the source of the measured multi-agent advantage.

</details>


### [130] [When Refusal Looks Safe: The Refusal-Cue Shortcut in Safety Guard Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03201)
*Yu Feng,Chunting Zang,Chen Shen,Rui Miao,Ge Teng,Weidong Cai,Jieping Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 安全防护模型（safety guards）用于过滤有害内容，通常通过在标注的提示-响应对上进行监督微调来训练。本文审计了WildGuardMix和GR-Train两个广泛使用的安全防护训练数据集，发现有害提示的响应中，拒绝表达几乎只与无害标签共存。这种不平衡导致了所谓"拒绝线索捷径"：在有害响应中插入拒绝线索可能使防护模型的判定从有害翻转为无害。该捷径不仅影响在这些数据集上训练的防护模型，也影响官方发布的LlamaGuard3和Qwen3Guard等模型（其训练数据未公开）。捷径在响应位置间持续存在，并且在同系列中较小的变体上通常更强。为了缓解这一问题，本文适配了稀疏互补掩码作为一种轻量级后验干预方法，无需重新训练即可识别并抑制少量与捷径相关的注意力头和MLP神经元。在两个主要基准上，该干预使拒绝线索引起的响应初始检测失败率相对降低了约79%，同时保持了标准检测性能。尽管优化时仅使用单一响应位置的线索，抑制效果可迁移到未见过的位置和数据集，表明不同位置上的捷径表现部分由共享的内部组件介导。进一步分析表明，捷径依赖与合法的拒绝识别在功能上部分可分离，抑制捷径广泛地保留了防护模型识别真正拒绝的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前广泛使用的安全防护训练数据集（WildGuardMix和GR-Train）存在有害提示响应中拒绝表达与无害标签几乎完全共现的不平衡现象，这可能导致防护模型学习到"拒绝线索捷径"——通过插入拒绝表达来翻转有害判定。这种捷径影响多个现有模型，需要被识别和缓解。

Method: 本文首先审计了WildGuardMix和GR-Train数据集，发现拒绝表达与无害标签的共现偏差。然后提出适配稀疏互补掩码（Sparse Complementary Masking）作为轻量级后验干预方法，通过识别并抑制少量与捷径相关的注意力头和MLP神经元来缓解拒绝线索捷径，无需重新训练模型。

Result: 在两个主要基准测试上，该后验干预使拒绝线索引起的响应初始检测失败率相对降低了约79%，同时保持了标准检测性能。抑制效果可迁移到未见过的响应位置和数据集。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了安全防护模型中的拒绝线索捷径问题，并提出了一种有效的轻量级后验干预方法。研究表明捷径依赖与合法的拒绝识别在功能上部分可分离，为未来构建更鲁棒的安全防护模型提供了方向。

Abstract: Safety guards are widely used to filter harmful content and are typically trained via supervised fine-tuning on labeled prompt-response pairs. We audit two widely used safety-guard training datasets, WildGuardMix and GR-Train, and find that among responses to harmful prompts, refusal expressions co-occur almost exclusively with unharmful labels. This imbalance motivates what we term the refusal-cue shortcut: inserting a refusal cue into a harmful response could flip the guard's verdict from harmful to unharmful. The shortcut affects not only guards trained on these datasets but also officially released models such as LlamaGuard3 and Qwen3Guard whose training data is undisclosed. It persists across response positions and is generally stronger in smaller variants within a family. To mitigate it, we adapt sparse complementary masking as a lightweight post-hoc intervention that identifies and suppresses a small set of shortcut-associated attention heads and MLP neurons without retraining. On two primary benchmarks, the intervention achieves an approximately 79% relative reduction in response-initial detection failures induced by refusal cues, while preserving standard detection performance. Although optimized using cues at a single response position, the suppression effect transfers to unseen positions and datasets, suggesting that shortcut manifestations across positions are partly mediated by shared internal components. Further analysis provides evidence that shortcut reliance and legitimate refusal recognition are partially functionally separable, as suppressing the shortcut broadly preserves the guard's ability to recognize genuine refusals.

</details>


### [131] [The Agent Operating System (AOS): A Reference Operating Architecture for Distributed Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03214)
*Ankur Sharma,Deep Shah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Agent Operating System (AOS)，一个供应商中立的参考操作架构，用于分布式代理系统，通过控制与治理平面和运行时与协调平面，实现意图管理、策略执行、信任与权威控制、审计性与可观察性等，以组合异构组件构建可治理、可靠、可观察和可互操作的代理系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代理框架、工作流引擎等技术改进了执行，但缺乏稳定、实现无关的操作架构来管理意图、选择能力、跨委托保持权威、控制不确定性、协调运行时行为以及重建重要行动的原因。

Method: AOS 包含内部两个平面：控制与治理平面（负责意图、策略、信任、权威、置信度、审计性、可观察性和人类监督）和运行时与协调平面（负责代理生命周期、工作流协调、模型与工具路由、上下文与记忆协调、调度、流量管理和运行时保障）。平台服务（Linux/Windows、容器运行时、物理基础设施）位于 AOS 边界之外，通过显式接口集成。

Result: 论文规范了 AOS 的概念、不变量、接口对象、优化目标、部署配置文件和可靠性职责，并识别了权衡和未解决的研究问题。

Conclusion: AOS 不是替代现有框架或基础设施，而是作为操作架构，使异构组件能够组合成可治理、可靠、可观察和可互操作的代理系统。

Abstract: Large language models have transformed artificial intelligence from isolated prediction services into components of long-running, distributed systems that reason, invoke tools, retrieve external state, delegate tasks, and act on behalf of users and organizations. The surrounding ecosystem has responded with agent frameworks, workflow engines, model-serving platforms, memory systems, communication protocols, and observability tools. These technologies improve execution, but they do not provide a stable, implementation-independent operating architecture for governing intent, selecting capabilities, preserving authority across delegation, controlling uncertainty, coordinating runtime behavior, and reconstructing why consequential actions occurred. This paper proposes the Agent Operating System (AOS), a vendor-neutral reference operating architecture for distributed agentic systems. AOS contains two internal planes: a Control & Governance Plane responsible for intent, policy, trust, authority, confidence, auditability, observability, and human oversight; and a Runtime & Coordination Plane responsible for agent lifecycle, workflow coordination, model and tool routing, context and memory coordination, scheduling, traffic management, and runtime assurance. Platform services, Linux or Windows, container runtimes, and physical infrastructure remain outside the AOS boundary and are integrated through explicit interfaces. The paper specifies AOS concepts, invariants, interface objects, optimization objectives, deployment profiles, and reliability responsibilities. It also identifies tradeoffs and unresolved research questions. AOS is not presented as a replacement for existing frameworks or infrastructure; it is proposed as the operating architecture through which heterogeneous components can be composed into governable, reliable, observable, and interoperable agentic systems.

</details>


### [132] [Reachability Is Not Realization: Tracing the Sources of LLM Benchmark Gains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03219)
*Yanchao Li,Wanhao Liu,Jiaqing Xie,Ben Gao,Yanbo Wang,Tianfan Fu,Yuqiang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出在固定预算、温度和答案格式下，对每个问题进行审计，区分"实现"（默认部署下模型正确回答）和"可达"（通过特定探测能在预算内找到答案）。研究发现：(1)随机路由在43个模型和任务设置中匹配或超越结构化搜索；(2)忽略答案的流程几乎无法保留增益；(3)沉默单个MLP块可修复68%-92%的预定义失败案例；(4)在5/6的匹配评估中，部署性能上升但可达上限持平或下降（如DAPO部署+14.7分，可达-13.3分）。结论：实现与可达并不总是同步变化，能力扩展声明应同时报告两者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基准测试的分数提升常被当作LLM能力增强的证据，但相同的分数提升可能反映不同的行为变化：模型可能获得了新答案，或只是产生了本就可及的答案。聚合分数无法逐问题区分这些变化，因此需要更细粒度的审计方法来理解能力变化的本质。

Method: 建立问题级别的审计框架，在固定预算、温度和答案格式下定义"实现"（默认部署产生正确答案）和"可达"（指定探测方法在预算内找到答案）。具体实验包括：(1)测试推理时层路由能否扩展可达性（随机路由vs结构化搜索）；(2)测试答案无关流程的增益保留；(3)沉默单个MLP块观察修复效果；(4)测试训练是否通过扩展可达性来缩小差距（匹配评估部署性能与可达上限的变化）。

Result: (1)随机路由在全部43个模型和任务设置中匹配或超越结构化搜索；(2)答案无关流程几乎不保留任何增益，需要访问正确答案；(3)沉默一个识别的MLP块可修复68%-92%的预定义失败集合；(4)在6个匹配评估中有5个出现部署性能上升而可达上限持平或下降的情况，其中DAPO部署分数上升14.7分但可达上限下降13.3分。

Conclusion: 实现和可达并不总是同步变化，仅凭部署性能提升不能直接推断出能力扩展。评估LLM能力变化时应同时报告在匹配评估条件下的实现性能和可达性，以便更准确地理解模型行为的真实变化。

Abstract: Benchmark gains are often treated as evidence of greater LLM capability. Yet the same gain can reflect different changes in model behavior. A model may reach new answers, or produce answers that were already within reach. Aggregate scores do not distinguish these changes question by question. We establish a question-level audit under fixed budgets, temperatures, and answer formats. A question is realized when the default deployment procedure produces the correct answer. A question is reachable when a specified probe finds that answer within a fixed budget. We first test whether inference-time layer routing can expand reachability. Under a matched budget, random routes match or exceed structured search in all 43 model and task settings. Answer-blind procedures retain almost none of this gain, which instead requires access to the correct answer. We then ask why reachable answers sometimes fail to appear. Across six cases spanning 0.5B to 31B, silencing one identified MLP block repairs 68 to 92 percent of a predefined failure set. We next test whether training closes the gap by expanding reachability. In five of six matched evaluations, deployed performance rises while the reachable ceiling remains flat or falls. For DAPO, the deployed score rises by 14.7 points while the reachable ceiling falls by 13.3 points. Across the settings we audit, realization and reachability therefore do not always change together. Claims of capability expansion should report both realized performance and reachability under matched evaluation conditions. Code is available at https://github.com/LiZaiyuan0619/reachability-not-realization

</details>


### [133] [UniNav: A Unified World-Action Diffusion Model for Visual Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03244)
*Changqing Zhou,Yueru Luo,Zeyu Jiang,Changhao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: UniNav是一个统一的世界-动作模型，通过单个扩散过程同时生成未来视觉观测和连续航点轨迹，用于图像目标视觉导航。它在一个Transformer中联合去噪视觉和航点标记，引入几何感知相机标记，并利用轨迹标注和纯视频数据进行训练，实现了高精度和低延迟（0.1秒一步推理）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有导航策略能高效预测航点轨迹但缺乏视觉前瞻能力，而导航世界模型能预测未来观测但需要昂贵的规划展开。作者希望将两者统一，既能预测未来观测又能生成动作轨迹，且避免高计算成本。

Method: UniNav采用扩散模型框架，将历史帧和目标图像作为条件，在一个Transformer中联合去噪视觉标记（未来观测）和航点标记（轨迹）。引入几何感知相机标记增强空间定位。训练时混合使用轨迹标注数据和纯视频数据（无需航点标注）。提供两个变体：UniNav-Full联合预测未来观测和轨迹，UniNav-Fast推理时移除未来图像标记以实现高效轨迹预测。

Result: 在导航基准测试中，UniNav在所有数据集上的ATE（平均轨迹误差）均优于最强基线。UniNav-Fast一步推理延迟仅0.1秒，且精度没有显著下降。

Conclusion: UniNav通过统一的世界-动作建模有效结合了未来预测与动作生成，在精度和效率上均取得优势，为视觉导航提供了一种新的范式。

Abstract: Image-goal visual navigation is a fundamental capability for embodied agents. Existing navigation policies efficiently predict waypoint trajectories but lack visual foresight, while navigation world models can anticipate future observations but often require costly planning rollouts. We present UniNav, a unified world-action model that generates future visual observations and continuous waypoint trajectories through a single diffusion process. Given history frames and a goal image, UniNav jointly denoises visual and waypoint tokens within a single transformer, unifying future prediction and action generation in a shared framework. To improve spatial grounding, we incorporate geometry-aware camera tokens. We also train on both trajectory-labeled navigation data and video-only data, enabling the model to benefit from diverse videos without waypoint annotations. Based on this unified framework, we introduce two variants: UniNav-Full jointly predicts interpretable future observations and their corresponding trajectories, while UniNav-Fast removes future-image tokens at inference for efficient trajectory prediction. Experiments on navigation benchmarks show that UniNav outperforms the strongest baseline in ATE across all datasets. With one-step inference, UniNav-Fast achieves a latency of 0.1s without a substantial accuracy drop. Code will be released.

</details>


### [134] [One Knob to Rule Them All: A Unified Optimal Transport View of Cold-Start Active Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03249)
*Ning Zhu,Xiaochuan Ma,Juntao Xu,Jingze Liang,Mengfei Zhao,An Chen,Liang-Jian Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于最优传输的冷启动主动学习框架ε-AS，通过熵正则化自适应选择数据，在多个数据集上达到最优性能，并在ImageNet-1k上提升准确率1.29%，减少选择时间56.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有冷启动主动学习方法依赖特定归纳偏置（如典型性、覆盖性、多样性），在不同任务上表现不一，缺乏自适应能力。真正的挑战不是设计新的选择启发式，而是让CSAL自动适应数据和任务。

Method: 通过最优传输视角，提出广义传输选择框架，揭示了现有方法的共享分配结构；引入理论分析表征熵正则化控制的权衡，建立任务无关的极小极大界；推导数据自适应正则化规则，提出基于Sinkhorn的ε-自适应选择（ε-AS）算法。

Result: 在六个公共数据集和多个标注预算下，ε-AS一致达到最先进性能。在ImageNet-1k上，平均准确率比ActiveFT提升1.29%，选择时间减少56.2%。

Conclusion: 提出的数据自适应正则化方法有效解决了冷启动主动学习中的自适应难题，实现了高效且鲁棒的样本选择。

Abstract: Cold-Start Active Learning (CSAL) aims to select a valuable subset from an unlabeled pool without any prior knowledge or human assistance. Existing methods take diverse routes based on typicality, coverage, or diversity. Each rests on its own inductive bias and therefore performs well on some tasks yet poorly on others. We argue that the real challenge is not to design yet another selection heuristic, but to make CSAL adapt automatically to the data and task at hand. To this end, we revisit CSAL through the lens of optimal transport. First, we propose a generalized transport selection framework that reveals the shared allocation structure of existing methods and exactly subsumes representative formulations. Second, we introduce a theoretical analysis that characterizes the trade-off controlled by entropic regularization and establishes a task-agnostic minimax bound for cold-start selection. These results provide a principled foundation for adapting the regularization strength to the unlabeled data. Third, we derive a data-adaptive regularization rule and present a novel Sinkhorn-based CSAL algorithm, termed $ε$-Adaptive Selection ($ε$-AS). Extensive experiments on six public datasets and multiple annotation budgets show that $ε$-AS consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance. On ImageNet-1k, it improves the average accuracy over ActiveFT by 1.29% while reducing selection time by 56.2%. Code will be released at https://github.com/Z-yiwei/OT-CSAL

</details>


### [135] [TaskPress: Query-Agnostic KV Cache Compression via Task-Guided Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03276)
*Wonpyo Park,Seung-won Hwang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TaskPress 提出一种任务引导、查询无关的 KV 缓存剪枝框架，利用高层任务指南作为元查询在预填充阶段过滤无关 token，并借助量化缩放因子作为零成本信号检测重要表征异常值，从而构建可跨不同查询复用的紧凑缓存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 KV 缓存剪枝方法通常为单一查询优化缓存，导致生成的缓存无法在未见过的查询间复用；随着序列长度线性增长，长上下文推理的缓存开销成为瓶颈。

Method: TaskPress 引入任务指南作为元查询，在预填充阶段根据任务语义过滤无关 token，实现查询无关的缓存剪枝；同时利用量化缩放因子作为零成本代理信号，识别表征中的异常值（即重要 token），从而高效构建可复用的紧凑缓存。

Result: 在多种长上下文任务上的实验表明，TaskPress 能够高效创建紧凑且可跨不同查询复用的 KV 缓存，在保持性能的同时显著降低缓存大小。

Conclusion: TaskPress 通过任务引导的、查询无关的剪枝策略，结合量化缩放因子的零成本重要性检测，为长上下文 LLM 推理提供了一种高效的缓存复用方案，解决了传统方法无法跨查询复用的问题。

Abstract: Long-context inference with large language models is constrained by the linear growth of the key-value cache to sequence length. While pruning offers mitigation, prevailing methods determine query-specific token importance that cannot be reused across unseen queries. In contrast, we introduce TaskPress, a framework for task-guided, query-agnostic KV cache eviction. Instead of optimizing the cache for a single query, TaskPress constructs a reusable memory representation conditioned on a high-level task guide. The guide functions as a meta-query during prefill to filter irrelevant tokens before downstream queries are issued. In addition, TaskPress leverages quantization scale factors as a zero-cost signal for detecting influential representation outliers, providing an efficient proxy for token importance. Experiments on conducted on various tasks with long context input demonstrate that TaskPress efficiently creates a compact, reusable cache across diverse queries.

</details>


### [136] [AgentPanel: Toward a New Paradigm for Human--AI Collaboration in Exploring Scientific Questions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03283)
*Zhiyao Cui,Qianyi Wang,Haoyang Yan,Yiqun Zhang,Siyue Ren,Hangfan Zhang,Zelin Tan,Hao Li,Chunjiang Mu,Dexian Cai,Shao Zhang,Chen Zhang,Meng Li,Jianan Chai,Yuting Fan,Zichao Ye,Xiaolei Yang,Xinyao Lu,Yuyang Yu,Wenjie Lou,Xiaosong Wang,Fenghua Ling,Shiyang Feng,Mao Su,Qiaosheng Zhang,Bo Zhang,Yang Chen,Lei Bai,Shuyue Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgentPanel 是一个多智能体论坛，通过异构智能体异步讨论和人类协作，支持科研探索中的想法生成、浏览、整理与总结，并在离线实验和人类评估中表现优于集中式多智能体辩论基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的科研想法探索依赖小型小组讨论或与单个大语言模型的一对一互动，这些方法通常只提供有限的视角和方向，缺乏多样性。因此需要一种能够提供更广泛视角和探索支持的平台。

Method: 提出 AgentPanel，一个多智能体论坛式人机协作平台。异构智能体在论坛环境中异步讨论科学问题，研究者可以提交问题、浏览和整理候选想法、与智能体进行后续交互，并可选地生成事后总结报告。平台通过离线实验和20名参与者的人类研究进行评估。

Result: 离线实验表明 AgentPanel 优于集中式多智能体辩论基线。人类研究显示用户重视 AgentPanel 的视角多样性和探索支持。在与常用 LLM 工具的体验比较中，65% 的参与者认为 AgentPanel 在研究方向的广度以及早期探索的总体适用性方面更优。

Conclusion: AgentPanel 有效地提供了多样化的视角和广泛的探索支持，在科研早期探索阶段具有实用价值，为人类与 AI 在科学探索中的协作提供了一种新的范式。

Abstract: Identifying promising scientific ideas remains an important challenge in research practice. Researchers commonly rely on small-group discussions or one-to-one interactions with a single large language model, yet these approaches often expose them to only a limited range of perspectives and directions. We present AgentPanel, a multi-agent forum for human--AI collaboration in scientific exploration. Heterogeneous agents asynchronously discuss scientific questions in a forum-style environment, while researchers can submit questions, browse and organize candidate ideas, engage agents in follow-up interactions, and optionally generate post-hoc summary reports. We evaluate AgentPanel in terms of idea quality, exploration breadth, interaction effectiveness, candidate-selection efficiency, and practical utility. Offline experiments show that AgentPanel outperforms a centralized multi-agent debate baseline. A human study with 20 participants further shows that users value AgentPanel for perspective diversity and exploration support. In experience-based comparisons with commonly used LLM tools, 65\% of participants favored AgentPanel for both breadth of research directions and overall suitability for early-stage exploration. The platform is publicly available at https://agentpanel.cc/.

</details>


### [137] [DocTrace: Towards Traceable Long Document VQA via Hierarchical Evidence Graph Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03292)
*Le Xiang,Zhicheng Guan,Hong Chen,Xiaocong Lin,Zhenghua Lei,Teng Hu,Bolei He,Long Zeng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DocTrace，将长文档视觉问答（LongDocVQA）转化为显式证据图推理问题，通过层次化框架逐步进行证据定位、结构化文档解析和证据图推理，并结合两阶段训练（联合SFT初始化+任务特定GRPO优化），在三个基准上显著超越现有方法，同时构建可追溯的证据图，实现透明可验证的推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法（端到端MLLM、RAG流水线、文档代理）缺乏显式机制来表示和验证推理过程中证据的渐进组合，限制了答案准确性和可追溯性。因此需要一种能够显式进行证据溯源的方法。

Method: 提出DocTrace层次化框架，包含证据定位、结构化文档解析和证据图推理三个渐进阶段；采用两阶段训练：联合监督微调（SFT）初始化能力，然后通过任务特定组相对策略优化（GRPO）和专门奖励进一步优化。

Result: 在MMLongBench-Doc、LongDocURL和SlideVQA三个基准上，DocTrace以Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct为骨干，分别取得14.4、11.3和11.7个百分点的绝对提升，持续优于现有开源基线及专有MLLM。

Conclusion: DocTrace不仅性能领先，还构建了具有显式节点级溯源的可追溯证据图，实现了长文档理解中透明且可验证的推理。

Abstract: Long Document Visual Question Answering (LongDocVQA) requires Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) to locate, integrate, and reason over heterogeneous document elements distributed across multiple pages. Existing approaches, including end-to-end MLLMs, retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) pipelines, and document agents, often lack explicit mechanisms to represent and verify how grounded evidence is progressively composed during reasoning, limiting both answer accuracy and traceability. In this paper, we cast LongDocVQA as an explicit evidence graph reasoning problem rather than implicit answer prediction. To this end, we propose DocTrace, a hierarchical framework that progressively performs evidence localization, structured document parsing, and evidence graph reasoning to enable explicit evidence provenance. To effectively learn these capabilities, we develop a two-stage training framework: joint Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) first initializes evidence localization and graph reasoning abilities, followed by task-specific Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with dedicated rewards to further optimize these capabilities. Extensive experiments on MMLongBench-Doc, LongDocURL, and SlideVQA demonstrate that DocTrace consistently outperforms both existing open-source baselines and proprietary MLLMs. Compared with the Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct backbone, DocTrace achieves absolute improvements of 14.4, 11.3, and 11.7 points on the three benchmarks, respectively. Beyond competitive performance, DocTrace constructs traceable evidence graphs with explicit node-level provenance, enabling transparent and verifiable reasoning for long document understanding.

</details>


### [138] [Distractor-Aware Truncation: Disentangling Context-Length Effects from Signal Loss in Long-Context LLM Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03297)
*Mohsen Arjmandi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文质疑了“在保留相关信息的前提下，较短上下文更好”的常见论断。通过在两个长上下文基准上使用不同截断协议（朴素截断与干扰感知截断）测试多种语言模型，发现朴素截断导致分数崩溃，而干扰感知截断则保持甚至提升性能。作者指出，未来研究必须明确如何区分信号与干扰，否则结果可能模糊不清。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献普遍认为，在保留相关信息的情况下，较短的上下文效果更好。作者希望检验这一论断是否成立，并揭示截断方式对评估结果的影响。

Method: 在BABILong和GraphWalks两个长上下文基准上，以四种上下文保留比例（100%、75%、50%、25%）和两种截断协议（朴素截断：删除中间内容；干扰感知截断：仅删除与任务无关的内容）测试了Claude系列（Haiku 4.5、Sonnet 4.6、Opus 4.7）及GPT-5.5。还在MRCR v2和Oolong基准上进行了验证。

Result: 朴素截断下，所有测试的分数均单调下降（配对Wilcoxon检验，Holm校正后p_adj < 0.05）。干扰感知截断下，性能保持或提升：较小的Claude模型在BABILong上有统计显著提升，较大模型（Opus 4.7和GPT-5.5）达到全上下文天花板。在GPT-5.5上复制了相同结果，排除了单一提供商的偶然性。机制分析显示，朴素截断在25%保留率时仅不到1%的样本包含答案内容，而干扰感知截断通过设计保留了答案。

Conclusion: 朴素截断并不是对上下文窗口效应的测量，而是对中间删除是否恰好保留答案的测量。未来关于上下文长度影响的研究必须明确说明如何区分信号与干扰，否则结果可能在两种相反假设之间模棱两可。

Abstract: A standard claim in the literature on retrieval-augmented and memory-augmented language models is that shorter context is better when the relevant information is preserved. We test this claim by running every sample of two long-context benchmarks -- BABILong and GraphWalks (BFS) -- at four context-retention fractions (100%, 75%, 50%, 25%) under two truncation protocols. The first is the naive protocol implicitly used in much prior work: drop content from the middle of the prompt. The second is distractor-aware: identify the task-relevant content for each sample and drop only the rest. We evaluate three sizes of the Claude family (Haiku 4.5, Sonnet 4.6, Opus 4.7) and, to test cross-provider generality, GPT-5.5 from a different provider; we apply the same protocol to two further benchmarks (MRCR v2, Oolong). Under naive truncation, score collapses monotonically (paired Wilcoxon, Holm-corrected p_adj < 0.05 in all eight BABILong and GraphWalks cells). Under the distractor-aware protocol -- which preserves the signal by construction -- performance is preserved or improves: the two smaller Claude models show statistically significant gains on BABILong, while the larger models (Opus 4.7 and GPT-5.5) sit at their full-context ceiling. The naive collapse and its distractor-aware recovery replicate on GPT-5.5, ruling out a single-provider artifact. The mechanism is direct: under the naive protocol the answer-bearing content survives in fewer than 1% of samples at 25% retention; under the distractor-aware protocol it is preserved by construction. The naive protocol is therefore not a measurement of context-window effects; it is a measurement of how often middle-removal happens to spare the answer. We conclude that future studies of context-length effects must specify how they distinguish signal from distractor, or they are at best ambiguous between two opposite hypotheses.

</details>


### [139] [SeaSlides: Semantic Abstraction Layer for Agentic Slide Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03298)
*Shengjun Fang,Chenyang Wu,Zongzhang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SeaSlides is an agentic slide generation framework that introduces a semantic abstraction layer, where models write structured content via reusable components and capability modules, while templates handle layout, style, and rendering. Implemented in HTML and Typst, it uses capability modules for equations, code, and charts, and three feedback stages to catch errors. Evaluated on a 128-task validation set and a new 32-task rich-content benchmark, SeaSlides produces more readable, content-oriented source than SVG-heavy generation and achieves the highest rich-content macro-average under most models.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing presentation generation systems either use templates (which preserve regularity but restrict adaptation) or free-form HTML/SVG (which gives flexibility but forces low-level rendering decisions, causing brittleness for technical slides with formulas, code, and graphics). There is a need for a system that balances content authoring flexibility with reliable rendering, especially for long technical decks.

Method: SeaSlides builds an agentic slide-generation framework around a semantic abstraction layer. The model writes structured slide content using reusable components and capability modules, while templates own layout, style, and rendering. It is instantiated in two backends: SeaSlides-HTML (template-defined DOM components) and SeaSlides-Typst (template functions and package modules). Capability modules route specialized content (equations, code, charts) to dedicated renderers. Three feedback stages localize build errors, project-constraint violations, and visual defects before export.

Result: Combined evaluation using the 128-task UltraPresent validation set and a new 32-task SeaSlidesBench-Rich benchmark (covering math, code, pseudocode, tables, charts, diagrams). Across four generation models, both SeaSlides backends produce more readable, content-oriented source than SVG-heavy generation. A SeaSlides backend attains the highest rich-content macro-average under three of the four models while maintaining competitive overall qualitative performance.

Conclusion: Semantic abstraction is a practical authoring principle for presentation generation across different backends. By decoupling content authoring from low-level rendering details via reusable components and template ownership, SeaSlides improves source readability, content fidelity, and robustness for technical slides.

Abstract: Agentic presentation generation must preserve source content, maintain coherent visual design, render specialized objects, and produce usable artifacts. Existing systems meet only part of this requirement: templates preserve regularity but restrict adaptation, whereas free-form HTML or SVG gives models flexibility at the cost of low-level rendering decisions. This mismatch makes long technical decks brittle, especially when slides contain formulas, code, or data graphics. We present SeaSlides, an agentic slide-generation framework built around a semantic abstraction layer. Rather than authoring coordinates, inline styles, or raw SVG geometry, the model writes structured slide content through reusable components and capability modules, while templates own layout, style, and rendering. We instantiate this principle separately in HTML and Typst: SeaSlides-HTML uses template-defined DOM components, whereas SeaSlides-Typst uses template functions and package-backed modules. Capability modules route equations, code, and charts to dedicated renderers, and three feedback stages localize build errors, project-constraint violations, and visual defects before export. The two systems retain backend-specific syntax and contracts while sharing the same authoring boundary. For evaluation, we combine the 128-task UltraPresent validation setting with SeaSlidesBench-Rich, a new 32-task benchmark stressing mathematics, code, pseudocode, tables, charts, and diagrams. Across four generation models, both SeaSlides backends produce more readable, content-oriented source than SVG-heavy generation. A SeaSlides backend attains the highest rich-content macro-average under three of the four models while maintaining competitive overall qualitative performance. These results support semantic abstraction as a practical authoring principle across presentation backends.

</details>


### [140] [Screenshots or Tools? Eliciting Tool Use and Managing Multimodal Context in Hybrid GUI-MCP Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03327)
*Siqi Fan,Minghao Li,Xiaoqian Ma,Wenhui Tan,Xiusheng Huang,Juntong Wu,Liujie Zhang,Shuo Shang,Weihang Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Hybrid computer-use agents combine screenshot-based interaction with text tool calls. The paper finds that tool availability alone does not guarantee improvement: it helps reasoning models (+4.0pp) but hurts non-reasoning ones (-5.9pp) due to poor tool-decision behavior. Even when reasoning models avoid obvious errors, they only use tools on 23.9% of tool-reachable tasks—a phenomenon termed the "adoption gap." The root cause is that models have cheaper alternatives and are never trained to use tools. Multi-turn RL with a dense tool bonus can steer behavior (spreadsheet adoption from 0.03→0.33) but does not improve held-out accuracy. At the context level, successful tool calls make subsequent screenshots redundant; dropping them halves image history, cutting tokens by ~1/3 with small accuracy loss. Retraining under the same observation rule eliminates that loss, yielding a compressed agent that achieves 37.8% accuracy vs 33.0% at 53% input cost, closing the rich-lean gap. Tools help only when the model chooses and integrates them; current hybrid agents leave many such choices unused.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To understand why hybrid computer-use agents (acting via screenshots and text tool calls) do not uniformly benefit from tool availability, and to identify the causes of tool under-utilization (the "adoption gap") across different model types.

Method: The authors use the OSWorld-MCP benchmark (309 tasks) with a GUI-MCP harness to compare reasoning vs non-reasoning models with identical tool access. They analyze tool-decision behavior, identify failure modes (ignore, misname, false termination), and measure the "adoption gap." They then probe the cause via multi-turn RL: at the action level, they apply a dense tool bonus; at the context level, they drop redundant screenshots after successful tool calls, halving image history and retraining under the same observation rule.

Result: Same MCP tools improve a reasoning model by +4.0pp but degrade a non-reasoning model by -5.9pp. The reasoning model still only calls tools on 55/309 tasks (23.9% of tool-reachable ones). Dense tool bonus raises spreadsheet adoption from 0.03 to 0.33 but held-out accuracy does not improve. Dropping redundant screenshots cuts tokens by ~1/3 with small accuracy loss; retraining removes that loss. The compressed agent reaches 37.8% vs 33.0% for the uncompressed one, at 53% input cost, and closes the rich-lean gap on a pre-registered degraded subset.

Conclusion: Tool availability is not enough; models must choose and integrate tools effectively. The adoption gap stems from models having cheaper alternatives and lacking training to use tools. Behavior is steerable via RL bonuses but competence (accuracy) is not easily improved. The bottleneck is tool-call semantics. Compressing the observation history by removing redundant screenshots after tool calls, combined with retraining, yields better efficiency and accuracy, closing the rich-lean gap.

Abstract: Hybrid computer-use agents can act through screenshots or call text tools. We find that having a tool available does not settle which way the effect goes. Under one identical GUI-MCP harness on the OSWorld-MCP benchmark (309 tasks), the same MCP tools improve a reasoning model by +4.0pp and degrade a non-reasoning model by -5.9pp (5 runs each, both beyond 2 SE). What separates the two is tool-decision behavior. The non-reasoning policy ignores, misnames, or falsely terminates around tools. The reasoning model avoids these failures, yet still calls a tool on only 55/309 tasks, 23.9% of the tool-reachable ones. We call this shortfall the adoption gap. Both levels of the problem share one cause: the model already has a cheaper route and is never trained to take it. Multi-turn RL probes that cause. At the action level, a dense tool bonus raises spreadsheet adoption 0.03 -> 0.33 and carries into greedy decoding, but held-out accuracy does not follow. Behavior is steerable; competence is not. The bottleneck lies in tool-call semantics. At the context level, a successful tool call often makes the next screenshot redundant. Dropping it and halving image history cuts input tokens by about a third, at a small accuracy cost. Retraining under the same observation rule removes that cost. The compressed agent then reaches 37.8% against 33.0% for the uncompressed operating point, at 53% of the input cost, and closes the rich-lean gap on a pre-registered degraded subset to zero. Tools help when the model chooses and integrates them, and current hybrid agents leave many such choices unused.

</details>


<div id='cs.MM'></div>

# cs.MM [[Back]](#toc)

### [141] [Caved or Convinced: Temporal Sampling Gates Claim Deference in Video Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03160)
*Yuxin Cao,Wei Song,Jingling Xue,Jin Song Dong*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本研究揭示视频大语言模型在时序顺序判断中因“可用性”（采样帧是否包含事件）和“加权性”（是否信任视觉证据胜过用户主张）两类原因，会产生“屈从假主张”和“拒绝真主张”两种相反错误。通过帧保持重排和采样偏移两种干预分离了二者，发现当事件未被采样时所有模型对真假主张接受率相同（Youden's J=0），且多数能读取顺序的模型仍会屈从假主张（加权上限）。为此提出反转测试：在不读取用户主张的情况下，正向和反向评分采样帧后决定回答、重采样或弃权，使能读取顺序的模型准确率达0.92-1.00，不能读取的模型则弃权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频大语言模型在回答“两个事件谁先发生”时可能犯两种相反错误：屈从于错误主张（sycophancy）或拒绝正确主张。已有工作仅针对前一种错误通过降低模型对用户的信任来缓解，但这会加剧后一种错误。此外，文献将这两种错误归因于同一原因，而本文指出它们实际源于两个独立因素：可用性（稀疏采样帧是否包含事件）和加权性（模型是否信任视觉证据胜过用户主张）。因此需要分离这两个因素并设计更全面的解决方案。

Method: 本文设计了两种干预来分离可用性和加权性：① 帧保持重排：交换两个事件在视频中的出现顺序（保持帧不变），从而翻转主张的真假；② 采样偏移：在固定帧预算下移动采样偏移，使得两个事件同时被捕获或同时被错过。当事件被错过时，两个孪生版本呈现完全相同帧，此时模型对真假主张的接受率应相等（Youden's J=0）。进而提出反转测试：不读取用户主张，而是对采样帧进行正向和反向评分，依据评分结果决定直接回答、重采样或弃权。

Result: 在9个视频LLM上的实验显示：当事件未被采样时，每个模型对真假主张的接受率完全相同（Youden's J=0），说明可用性是必要条件但非充分条件。其中5个模型能读取顺序，但其中4个仍然屈从于假主张，表明其加权存在上限（即使有证据也选择信任用户）。反转测试使能读取顺序的模型准确率提升至0.92-1.00，不能读取顺序的模型则选择弃权而非猜测。

Conclusion: 信任无法在从未被采样的证据上校准。视频LLM的两种时序错误分别源于可用性和加权性，两者必须分别处理。反转测试通过不读取用户主张、仅依靠视频帧的正反向评分来决策，能有效缓解两类错误，为视频时序推理提供了一种无需修改模型信任度的可靠方法。

Abstract: When asked which of two events came first, video large language models can fail in two opposite ways: cave to a false claim, or reject a true one. Prior video sycophancy work measures only the first and mitigates it by teaching the model to trust the user less, a fix known in text and image models to worsen the second. In video, both failures come from two causes the literature treats as one: availability, whether the sparse sampled frames contain the two events, and weighting, whether that evidence is trusted over the user. We separate them with two interventions that keep the claim fixed: a frame-preserving reorder that flips the claim's truth, and a sampling-offset shift that captures or misses both events at a fixed frame budget. When the events are missed, the two twins present identical frames, so each of the nine models we evaluate accepts a true and a false claim at the same rate, making Youden's $J=0$ by construction. Availability is necessary but not sufficient. Five of the nine read the order, yet four of those five still cave to the false claim, so their deference hits a weighting ceiling. Since trust cannot be calibrated over evidence that was never sampled, we propose a reversal test that cancels the model's order prior by scoring the sampled frames forward and reversed, then answers, resamples, or abstains without reading the claim. The test raises the order accuracy to 0.92-1.00 on the models that read the order and abstains rather than guesses on those that cannot.

</details>


### [142] [Hear to See: Discerning Stateful Listening for Audio-Visual Instance Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03264)
*Leiye Liu,Miao Zhang,Jiahong Jiang,Jingjing Li,Jialong Zhong,Kai Peng,Tingwei Liu,Wei Ji,Yongri Piao,Huchuan Lu*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 提出Hear to See (H2S)方法，通过声学-语义投影器（ASP）解耦混合音频并建立层次化语义-空间对应，以及异步动态调制器（ADM）利用音频调制的Mamba自适应调整状态转移，在AVISeg数据集上实现48.54 mAP的SOTA性能，超越此前方法7.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音频-视觉实例分割方法难以匹配重叠声学事件与视觉实例，且无法处理音频-视觉信号的异步动态。需要建立声源与视觉实例的精确对应，并在时间未对齐时保持鲁棒跟踪。

Method: 提出H2S框架，包含两个核心模块：(1) 声学-语义投影器（ASP）解耦混合音频信号，建立从语义到空间域的层次化对应；(2) 异步动态调制器（ADM）通过音频调制的Mamba机制，在动态变化时优先当前信息，在稳定阶段保持连续性，自适应调整状态转移。

Result: 在AVISeg数据集上，H2S使用COCO预训练的ResNet50达到48.54 mAP，超越此前最佳方法7.8%，取得SOTA性能。

Conclusion: H2S有效解决了重叠声源对应和异步时序匹配两个核心挑战，为音频-视觉实例分割提供了新的解决方案，代码将开源。

Abstract: Audio-visual instance segmentation (AVIS) requires accurately identifying and tracking individual sounding objects with pixel-level masks. Existing methods struggle to match overlapping acoustic events with visual instances and handle asynchronous audio-visual dynamics. Therefore, two critical questions arise: how can a model establish precise correspondence between overlapping sound sources and visual instances, and how can a model maintain robust tracking when audio and visual signals are temporally misaligned?This paper proposes Hear to See (H2S), addressing these challenges through two mechanisms. The Acoustic-Semantic Projector (ASP) disentangles mixed audio and establishes hierarchical correspondence from semantic to spatial domains. The Asynchronous Dynamics Modulator (ADM) adaptively adjusts state transitions via audio-modulated Mamba, prioritizing current information during dynamic variations and maintaining continuity in stable periods.Experiments on AVISeg show H2S achieves SOTA performance, attaining 48.54 mAP with a COCO pretrained ResNet50 and surpassing the previous by 7.8\%. The code will be open-sourced once the paper is accepted. The source code will be publicly available at https://github.com/leiyeliu/H2S.

</details>


### [143] [Balancing Efficiency and Efficacy: Training-Free Attention-Guided Switching Between Explicit and Latent Thoughts for MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03450)
*Haoqian Kang,Liupeng Li,Kuofeng Gao,Jinpeng Wang,Zhenyu Lu,Bin Chen,Ke Chen,Yaowei Wang*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练的多模态大语言模型推理策略AGS，通过解耦感知与推理过程，利用视觉-文本注意力比动态切换隐式推理与显式文本生成，在提升准确率的同时显著降低计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态大语言模型推理方法存在瓶颈：显式文本链式推理计算成本高且易产生视觉幻觉；隐式推理需要昂贵的训练；直接迁移无训练的LLM推理机制到多模态场景效果不稳定，因为其依赖的词元级熵会混淆视觉感知模糊性与逻辑推理不确定性。

Method: 提出一种无需训练的推理策略AGS，核心是设计了一个新的度量指标——视觉-文本注意力比，用于动态评估模型的认知焦点。基于该指标，AGS自适应地触发：对感知型词元进行隐式推理以保留连续空间中的高保真视觉信息，对逻辑型词元则强制执行显式文本生成以保持结构锚定。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法在多个基准上达到最先进性能，通过减少自回归步数和延迟，显著提升了准确率和推理效率。

Conclusion: AGS通过解耦感知与推理，使用注意力比指导动态切换，有效解决了多模态推理中视觉幻觉与计算效率的权衡问题，为无需训练的多模态推理提供了新范式。

Abstract: Reasoning in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) requires both fine-grained visual perception and rigorous logical deduction. Explicit text-based Chain-of-Thought (CoT) is computationally expensive and prone to visual hallucinations, while existing latent reasoning methods typically require costly training. Furthermore, directly adapting training-free LLM reasoning mechanisms to the multimodal setting yields unstable performance. We identify that this failure stems from their reliance on token-level entropy, which fundamentally conflates perceptual ambiguity (e.g., unclear visual details) with logical uncertainty (e.g., complex reasoning steps). To overcome this bottleneck, we present a novel training-free inference strategy for MLLMs that explicitly decouples perception and reasoning. We propose a novel metric, the vision-to-text attention ratio, to dynamically gauge the model's cognitive focus. Guided by this metric, our proposed framework, Attention-Guided Switching (AGS), adaptively triggers latent reasoning for perceptual tokens to preserve high-fidelity visual information in the continuous space, while enforcing explicit text generation for logical tokens to maintain structural anchoring. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance, significantly improving both accuracy and inference efficiency by reducing autoregressive steps and latency. Code is released at https://github.com/swordAndSnow/MM26-AGS.

</details>


### [144] [Adaptive Modality Reliability Diagnosis and Restoration for Robust Multimodal Intent Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03475)
*Suraj Kumar,Mohnish Raj,Soumi Chattopadhayay,Chandranath Adak,Ayan Dutta*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: PRIME 是一个闭环可靠性引导框架，通过诊断、修复和重新评估模态质量来处理多模态意图识别中模态的噪声、缺失、语义冲突和失衡问题。它利用上下文对数方差估计模态弱点，使用原型条件变分恢复模块从互补模态重建退化表示，并在恢复后重新评估可靠性，最后通过逆方差融合进行预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法隐式推断模态重要性并重新加权或抑制不可靠输入，但无法确定退化模态是否可以被修复并重新信任。多模态意图识别中模态可能带有噪声、缺失、语义冲突或不成比例的主导，需要一个能闭环诊断、修复和重新评估模态质量的框架。

Method: PRIME 包含三部分：(1) 通过上下文对数方差估计模态弱点，利用预测置信度、认知分歧、跨模态一致性和特征退化等互补诊断证据；(2) 使用原型条件变分恢复模块从互补模态重建退化表示，由估计的弱点控制；(3) 恢复后重新评估可靠性，并将修复后的精度用于逆方差多模态融合。使用受控模态退化和异方差不确定性目标训练估计器。

Result: 在多模态意图识别基准上，PRIME 在干净数据下保持竞争力，同时在缺失、噪声、冲突和模态不平衡条件下提高了鲁棒性。

Conclusion: PRIME 提出了一种新的可靠性引导框架，不仅诊断和抑制不可靠模态，还能尝试修复并重新信任它们，实现了更鲁棒的多模态融合。

Abstract: Multimodal intent recognition combines linguistic, acoustic, and visual evidence, but individual modalities may be noisy, missing, semantically conflicting, or disproportionately dominant. Existing methods typically infer modality importance implicitly and either reweight or suppress unreliable inputs, without determining whether a degraded modality can be repaired and subsequently trusted. We propose PRIME (Precision-weighted Reliability Inference and Modality rEstoration), a closed-loop reliability guided framework that jointly diagnoses, restores, and reassesses modality quality at the sample level. PRIME represents the weakness of each modality through a contextual log-variance estimated from complementary diagnostic evidence, including predictive confidence, epistemic disagreement, cross-modal consensus, and feature degeneracy. Because modality-reliability annotations are unavailable, the estimator is explicitly trained using controlled modality corruption with known degradation severity, together with a heteroscedastic uncertainty objective. Rather than directly discarding an unreliable modality, PRIME uses its estimated weakness to control a prototype-conditioned variational restoration module that reconstructs the degraded representation from complementary modalities. Crucially, reliability is re-estimated after restoration, allowing the model to determine whether the repaired representation has become sufficiently trustworthy to contribute to prediction. The resulting post-restoration precisions are used for inverse-variance multimodal fusion. Experiments on multimodal intent-recognition benchmarks show that PRIME maintains competitive clean-data performance while improving robustness under missing, noisy, conflicting, and modality-imbalanced conditions.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [145] [Deep Divide-and-Reduce in Symbolic Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02628)
*Yusong Deng,Yanjie Li,Weijun Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种名为深度分治约简符号回归（DDRSR）的方法，通过严格的数学推导拓展了表达式分解与约简的适用范围，避免了暴力搜索子结构，在表达式分解和数值回归任务上均取得显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有符号回归的机器学习方法缺乏对表达式内在数学与物理原理的深刻理解；先驱方法AI Feynman虽利用了数据背后的数学性质，但其表达式简化机制适用范围窄、在复杂方程上易失败，且依赖暴力搜索子表达式，实用性受限。

Method: 基于严格的数学推导与证明，提出深度分治约简符号回归（DDRSR），从根本上拓宽表达式分解与约简的适用性，避免暴力搜索子结构，保证更广的通用性与严格的理论正确性。

Result: 经验评估表明，DDRSR在表达式分解和数值回归任务上均取得了显著优势。

Conclusion: 讨论了该范式的适用场景与内在局限性，并展望了未来研究方向。

Abstract: Symbolic regression (SR) is the task of discovering underlying patterns from data and representing them using mathematical expressions. Current machine learning approaches to SR often lack a profound understanding of the intrinsic mathematical and physical principles governing these expressions. While the pioneering AI Feynman method leverages the mathematical properties underlying the data, its expression simplification mechanism suffers from a narrow scope of applicability and is prone to failure on complex equations. Furthermore, its underlying mechanisms rely heavily on brute-force searches for sub-expressions, severely limiting its practical utility. Through rigorous mathematical deduction and proofs, we propose our method, Deep Divide and Reduce in Symbolic Regression (DDRSR). DDRSR fundamentally broadens the applicability of expression decomposition and reduction, circumvents the need for brute-force sub-structure searches, and ensures both wider versatility and strict theoretical correctness. Empirical evaluations demonstrate that these theoretical principles yield significant advantages in both expression decomposition and numerical regression tasks. Finally, we discuss the applicable scenarios and inherent limitations of this paradigm, alongside promising directions for future research.

</details>


### [146] [Multimodal Auto-regressive Transformer Surrogate for Modeling Variable Operations and Quantifying Uncertainty in Geological Carbon Storage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02629)
*Yifu Han,Louis J. Durlofsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文开发了一个多模态自回归Transformer代理模型，用于在地质不确定条件下模拟CO2地质封存操作（变射孔和注入策略）。代理模型处理3D地质模型、相对渗透率标量和控制变量三种模态，通过Transformer编码器融合并自回归预测饱和度和压力等关键量。在SEAM CO2模型上训练后，该代理在测试集上达到中位饱和度MAE 0.028、相对误差0.2-5%，并能捕获注入控制模式切换。结合层次化MCMC数据同化，该代理有效降低了断层渗透率等关键参数的不确定性，后验预测与真实模型一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变井射孔和注入策略可提升地质碳封存效率，但传统模拟方法计算成本高，难以高效处理地质不确定性下的优化与数据同化问题。因此需要开发一种计算高效的代理模型来替代昂贵的高保真流模拟。

Method: 提出一种多模态自回归Transformer代理模型：使用三个独立编码器分别处理3D地质模型（卷积编码器）、相对渗透率标量参数（全连接编码器）和控制变量（全连接编码器），通过自注意力机制融合后，由时序解码器通过编码器-解码器交叉注意力进行自回归预测。模型使用4000个GEOS流模拟训练，输出监测点饱和度和压力、总注入和活动CO2质量以及饱和度足迹。

Result: 在随机采样的地质模型和控制变量测试集上，代理模型取得中位饱和度MAE 0.028，其他关键量的中位相对误差0.2-5%。模型成功捕获从定速率到定井底压力的控制模式切换。在层次化MCMC数据同化中，断层渗透率等关键元参数的不确定性显著降低，后验预测与真实模型结果一致。

Conclusion: 所提出的多模态Transformer代理模型能高效且准确地模拟变射孔和注入策略下的CO2地质封存动态，在不确定性量化与数据同化任务中表现优异，有望推动大规模碳封存操作的设计与优化。

Abstract: The use of variable well perforation and injection strategies can improve the efficiency of geological carbon storage operations. We develop a new multimodal auto-regressive transformer surrogate to model these operations under geological uncertainty. A modified SEAM CO2 geomodel, which involves a faulted system with three stacked aquifers, is considered. The two injection wells are perforated in stages, from bottom to top, with the stage durations and individual well injection rates treated as control variables. The surrogate model processes three input modalities - the 3D geomodel, scalar parameters characterizing relative permeability functions, and control variables - through separate encoders. These are fused via self-attention in a transformer encoder, and a temporal decoder generates predictions auto-regressively through encoder-decoder cross-attention. The surrogate is trained, using 4000 GEOS flow simulations, to predict saturation and pressure at monitoring locations, total injected and mobile CO2 mass, and saturation footprints. For a new test set, involving randomly sampled geomodels and control variables, the surrogate achieves a median saturation MAE of 0.028 and median relative errors of 0.2-5% for the other quantities of interest. Importantly, it captures the switch from rate to bottom-hole-pressure control. The surrogate model is used within a hierarchical Markov chain Monte Carlo data assimilation procedure for a synthetic true model under three operational strategies. Substantial uncertainty reduction is achieved for key metaparameters, particularly the fault permeabilities. Posterior predictions for saturation footprints and total injected and mobile CO2 mass are also shown to be generally consistent with true model results.

</details>


### [147] [LLMs Can Annotate Attribution Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02632)
*Ameen Patel,Max Zhang,Nathan Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自动流水线，利用语言模型将电路追踪中的特征或MLP神经元分组为超节点，替代耗时的手动标注环节。实验表明自动生成的超节点与人工标注同样可解释，并在两跳首都任务中成功恢复中间跳超节点。此外，还展示了用于开放性探索的概念验证，自动注释1000个维基百科补全的归因图并由LLM法官标记值得人工审查的图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电路追踪技术虽能揭示语言模型的内部计算，但需要大量手动操作来将特征或MLP神经元分组为超节点，这一步骤耗时且阻碍了可扩展性。本文旨在通过自动化这一分组过程来提升电路追踪的效率。

Method: 提出一个简单流水线：直接将特征描述输入语言模型，由其自动分组生成超节点。使用自动化可解释性指标评估分组质量，并在两跳首都任务中验证超节点恢复能力。同时，在维基百科提示补全的1000个归因图上进行开放探索，用LLM法官标记值得人工审查的图。

Result: 自动化流水线生成的超节点在可解释性上与人工标注相当；在两跳首都任务的100个提示中，有97个成功恢复了对应中间跳的超节点；开放探索概念验证中，能自动注释大量归因图并筛选出值得关注的图。

Conclusion: 即使简单的自动化也能生成有意义的归因图注释，从而推动自动电路追踪的进一步发展。

Abstract: Circuit tracing is an exciting technique for revealing the internal computation of language models, but it requires a time-intensive manual step of grouping individual features or MLP neurons into supernodes. We present a simple pipeline for automating this step: directly presenting feature descriptions to a language model that groups them into supernodes. Using automated interpretability metrics, we confirm that supernodes generated by our pipeline are as interpretable as those generated by human annotators. On a two-hop Capitals task, our pipeline recovers a supernode corresponding to the intermediate hop in 97 of 100 prompts. Finally, we present a simple proof of concept using our pipeline for open-ended exploration, where we automatically annotate 1000 attribution graphs from Wikipedia prompt completions and then use an LLM judge to flag interesting graphs worth human review. We hope this work demonstrates that even simple automation can produce meaningful attribution graph annotations, motivating further work on automated circuit tracing.

</details>


### [148] [GeoID-PINN: Identifiability-Aware Regional Epidemic Inference with Geographic Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02633)
*Weixiong Hua,Fan Bu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 GeoID-PINN，一种基于物理信息神经网络的 SIRD 动力学模型，通过行随机源组成矩阵刻画空间依赖关系，并引入距离、邻接、通勤或超前滞后等空间先验进行正则化。模拟和真实 COVID-19 数据实验表明，准确的传播轨迹并不意味着能够正确恢复区域依赖结构，因此在使用前必须进行先验敏感性和观测模型检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区域监测数据同时反映本地传播、病例报告、输入病例和外部感染压力，这些因素难以单独识别。现有方法无法有效分离这些混杂的时空信息，需要一种能够区分区域间依赖结构和传播动态的建模框架。

Method: GeoID-PINN 采用物理信息神经网络（PINN）建模 SIRD 动力学，其中空间依赖关系由一个行随机源组成矩阵（row-stochastic source-composition matrix）表示，其每一行赋予非负的源权重且总和为1。该矩阵通过正则化项向基于距离、邻接、通勤或超前滞后信息构建的空间先验靠拢。模型同时拟合感染、康复和死亡轨迹，并估计传播尺度参数。

Result: 在四区域模拟中，与真实先验匹配时源组成误差为 0.099，无正则化时升至 0.159，强错误先验下达到 0.577，但轨迹拟合和传播尺度估计相似。在路易斯安那州 64 个县的真实 COVID-19 数据上，GeoID-PINN 相比自回归负二项基线将 MSE 从 32,957 降至 11,468，MAE 从 70.60 降至 57.73；但基线的负对数似然更低（5.158 vs 5.346），表明分布拟合更优。在 15 个县的对照分析中，邻接先验使 MSE 降低 6.85%、MAE 降低 3.1%。不同先验下的相似性能表明结构化正则化有效，但无法唯一确定边权重。

Conclusion: 准确预测传播轨迹并不保证能正确恢复区域间依赖结构。模型结果必须经过先验敏感性和观测模型检验才能进行因果解释。GeoID-PINN 提供了一种可正则化的空间依赖建模框架，但实际应用中需要谨慎选择先验并验证其稳健性。

Abstract: Regional surveillance data reflect local transmission, reporting, seeding, and external infection pressure, which are difficult to identify separately. We introduce GeoID-PINN, a physics-informed neural network (PINN) for susceptible-infectious-recovered-deceased (SIRD) dynamics. The model represents spatial dependence with a row-stochastic source-composition matrix whose rows assign nonnegative source weights that sum to one. We regularize this matrix toward a spatial prior constructed from distance, adjacency, commuting, or lead-lag information. In a four-region simulation with known truth, a compatible distance prior gives source-composition error 0.099. The error rises to 0.159 without regularization and 0.577 under a strongly misspecified prior, while trajectory fit and transmission-scale estimates remain similar. Accurate trajectories therefore do not guarantee recovery of the regional dependence structure. We also evaluate GeoID-PINN retrospectively using COVID-19 data from 64 Louisiana counties. Relative to an autoregressive negative-binomial baseline, Forecast-Trained Geo-PINN reduces mean squared error (MSE) from 32,957 to 11,468 and mean absolute error (MAE) from 70.60 to 57.73. The baseline has lower negative log likelihood (NLL), 5.158 versus 5.346, indicating better distributional fit but worse point accuracy. In a controlled 15-county comparison, county adjacency reduces MSE by 6.85 percent and MAE by 3.1 percent. Similar performance across plausible priors supports structured regularization but not unique edge recovery. These results require prior-sensitivity and observation-model checks before interpretation.

</details>


### [149] [Verifier-Guided Model Discovery for Physical Dynamical Systems with Pretrained Symbolic Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02662)
*Farbod Faraji,Francesco Belardinelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种验证器引导（VG）的工作流，以ODEFormer为符号骨干，从高维物理数据中发现符号形式的控制方程，在范德波尔振荡器和涡街尾流问题中验证了其可解释性与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性物理系统的可靠预测对科学发现和工程决策至关重要，但高保真仿真成本过高，机器学习代理模型不透明且泛化性受限。预训练Transformer虽可将合成ODE轨迹映射为方程，但难以可靠地迁移到高维物理数据。

Method: 构建验证器引导（VG）工作流，以ODEFormer为符号发现骨干，利用动力学和物理可接受性准则，从多轨迹候选方程池中筛选最优符号表达式，实现从低维合成数据到高维物理数据的迁移。

Result: 在范德波尔振荡器上，VG在未见初始条件下优于原始ODEFormer。针对涡街尾流现象，VG在固定和变雷诺数下通过坐标约简和符号发现，得到固定参数的降阶方程，恢复了基本涡脱振荡器及其高次谐波，无需尾流特定候选库或预设NS方程结构；跨参数模型可泛化到未见的雷诺数区间。

Conclusion: 建立了验证器引导的神经到符号方法论，为自然科学中可解释且可物理审计的预测提供了有效途径，并揭示了重建保真度与符号可发现性之间的非直接对应关系。

Abstract: Reliable forecasting of nonlinear physical systems underpins scientific discovery and engineering decision-making. Yet high-fidelity simulations are prohibitively costly, and machine-learning surrogates can be opaque and encode assumptions about system dynamics, limiting generalizability. Pretrained transformers mapping synthetic ODE trajectories to equations offer interpretable alternatives, promising transfer without system-specific equation knowledge. Transferring them reliably to high-dimensional physical data, however, remains an open challenge. We develop a verifier-guided (VG) workflow around ODEFormer as a symbolic backbone, using dynamical and physical-admissibility criteria to select from a multi-trajectory candidate equation pool, enabling transfer. On canonical Van der Pol oscillators, VG outperforms the original ODEFormer workflow across held-out initial conditions. We then address vortex shedding, a phenomenon occurring in atmospheric and plasma systems of societal relevance, through coordinate reduction and symbolic discovery at fixed and varying Reynolds numbers. VG discovers fixed-parameter reduced-order equations that recover the fundamental shedding oscillator and higher harmonics without a wake-specific candidate library or prescribed Navier-Stokes structure, while the cross-parameter model generalizes to withheld regimes. Reconstruction fidelity alone did not determine symbolic discoverability, highlighting the importance of compatibility between latent dynamics and the backbone's pretraining distribution. This work establishes a verifier-guided neural-to-symbolic methodology for interpretable and physically auditable forecasting in the natural sciences.

</details>


### [150] [CT-HEG: A Bidirectional, Timestamp-Attributed Event Graph for ICU In-Hospital Mortality Prediction - An Architectural Ablation Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02663)
*Mohammad Nasir Uddin,Rahnuma Tabassum Orpita,Asaduzzaman Anik,Eklachur Rahman Bhuiyan,Marjahan Risalat,SM Wali Ullah,Asif Ahamed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出连续时间异质图（CT-HEG）框架用于不规则ICU时序数据建模，并基于异质图注意力网络（CHIRP-Net）进行死亡率预测，在MIMIC-IV上达到AUROC 0.8618。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有序列模型能处理不规则采样但忽略实体间的类型化关系结构，而图模型通常假设固定时间间隔输入。ICU数据包含多种实体类型（就诊、生命体征、实验室事件）且时间戳不规则，需要一种既能建模不规则时间又能利用异质关系结构的方案。

Method: 构建连续时间异质EHR图（CT-HEG），每个ICU停留表示为带类型和时间戳的图，包含三种节点（visit, vital, lab_event）和2D边属性（归一化时间差t_hours/48和归一化值value_norm），无需插值。实例化为CHIRP-Net，四层异质GATv2Conv网络，在MIMIC-IV v3.1（31,142条ICU停留，LOS>=48h，13.4%死亡率）上评估，与逻辑回归、mTAND、Transformer、GRU-D对比，并进行消融实验。

Result: CHIRP-Net五种子平均AUROC 0.8449±0.0071（AUPRC 0.4958±0.0209）；集成模型AUROC 0.8618（95% CI: 0.8485-0.8745）。去除反向边使观察节点与就诊读出断开，AUROC下降0.1968±0.0073。时间注意力边特征贡献0.0247±0.0093 AUROC。合并异质边类型为一种关系（参数减少7倍）在所有种子上表现优于完整模型。校准后ECE为0.0307。

Conclusion: CT-HEG框架适用于不规则EHR数据，双向连接是模型利用输入的必要条件，合并边类型能提升性能。模型经温度缩放后校准良好。但外部验证、预先指定的时序评估和人口统计公平性审计仍是必要的。

Abstract: Accurate ICU mortality prediction requires modeling irregular clinical observations across heterogeneous entity types. Existing sequence models handle irregular sampling but ignore typed relational structure; existing graph models assume fixed-interval inputs. We introduce the Continuous-Time Heterogeneous EHR Graph (CT-HEG) schema and evaluate which architectural choices drive predictive performance. CT-HEG encodes each ICU stay as a typed, timestamped graph with three node types (visit, vital, lab_event) and 2D edge attributes (t_hours/48, value_norm) encoding timing and value without imputation. We instantiate CT-HEG as CHIRP-Net, a four-layer heterogeneous GATv2Conv network, evaluated on MIMIC-IV v3.1 (31,142 ICU stays, LOS>=48h, 13.4% mortality) with five seeds and bootstrapped confidence intervals, against logistic regression, mTAND, a Transformer, and GRU-D, plus an ablation study. CHIRP-Net achieved 5-seed mean AUROC 0.8449+/-0.0071 (AUPRC 0.4958+/-0.0209); the ensemble achieved AUROC 0.8618 (95% CI: 0.8485-0.8745). Removing reverse edges disconnected observation nodes from the visit readout, cutting AUROC by 0.1968+/-0.0073. Time-attentive edge features contributed 0.0247+/-0.0093 AUROC. Collapsing heterogeneous edge types into one relation (7x fewer parameters) outperformed the full model on all seeds. Post-calibration ECE was 0.0307. Temporal and demographic subgroup analyses were explored but not reported here, pending follow-up work. Bidirectional connectivity was necessary for the model to use its inputs at all, and CT-HEG was reasonably well calibrated after validation-fitted temperature scaling. These results support CT-HEG for irregular EHR data, while external validation, a pre-specified temporal evaluation, and a demographic fairness audit remain necessary before any claim of robustness. Code: https://github.com/nasiruddinstudents-ctrl/chirp-net-mimic-iv.

</details>


### [151] [Sphere Retraction Normalizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02668)
*Jie Zhang,Cheng-Fang Su,Yi-Jui Huang,Min-Te Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示在超球面残差流中，所有retraction映射退化为单一的标量设计选择（即如何将更新幅度转换为旋转角度），并由此提出了一族参数化的p-SpheretNorm方法（包含Proj-SpheretNorm和Cay-SpheretNorm），实验表明指数映射并非最优选择，有限p值能取得更好效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Geodesic Normalization (GeoNorm) 使用黎曼指数映射来保持隐藏状态的L2范数恒定，但指数映射仅是retraction映射族中的一个特例；作者希望探索更广泛的retraction映射在超球面残差流中的表现，并统一理解欧几里得残差连接与GeoNorm。

Method: 首先证明在超球面上所有retraction映射可简化为一个标量设计选择：即如何将更新的模长转换为旋转角度。基于此框架，实例化度量投影retraction和Cayley retraction得到Proj-SpheretNorm和Cay-SpheretNorm，它们属于单参数族p-SpheretNorm，其旋转角度会饱和而非无界增长。p=1和p=2分别对应上述两种方法，而恒等映射和GeoNorm作为极限情况出现。

Result: 在nanoGPT上，所有三种方法（Proj-SpheretNorm, Cay-SpheretNorm, p-SpheretNorm）均优于现有的轻量级深度连接方案，且最佳验证损失出现在有限p值处，表明指数映射并非球形残差流的首选，而只是谱系的一端。

Conclusion: 超球面残差流中的retraction映射选择本质上是一个标量角度转换问题；p-SpheretNorm族提供了比指数映射更灵活的选择，有限p值可获得更好性能。

Abstract: Residual connections are the de facto mechanism for training deep neural networks stably. Geodesic Normalization (GeoNorm) recasts them on a Riemannian manifold, orthogonalizing each layer output against the current hidden state and applying the resulting update through the Riemannian exponential map. Every hidden state thus keeps a constant $\ell_{2}$-norm, confining the residual stream to a hypersphere. The exponential map, however, is only one member of a broad family of retraction maps. We show that on the hypersphere this entire family collapses to a single scalar design choice. What distinguishes one retraction from another is only how the magnitude of an update is converted into a rotation angle within the plane spanned by the hidden state and the update. This view places Euclidean residual connections and GeoNorm in one framework. Instantiating it with the metric projection retraction and the Cayley retraction yields Proj-SpheretNorm and Cay-SpheretNorm, which are exactly norm-preserving yet require only algebraic operations. Both prove to be members of a one-parameter family of angular retractions, $p$-SpheretNorm, whose rotation angle saturates rather than growing without bound. The two methods above are recovered exactly at $p = 1$ and $p = 2$, while the identity map and GeoNorm arise only as limits at either end. On nanoGPT, all three methods outperform existing lightweight deep connection schemes, and the best validation loss is attained at finite $p$, indicating that the exponential map is not the preferred retraction for spherical residual streams but merely one end of a spectrum.

</details>


### [152] [Learning Molecular Representations from Cellular Phenotypes with Structure Preservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02688)
*Xuan Lin,Jingyu Sheng,Tengfei Ma,Li Sun,Dapeng Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了 PhenMol，一种保持分子结构的多模态表示学习框架，通过将分子和细胞表征分解为共享与私有成分，在利用细胞表型信息进行对齐的同时保留分子化学空间的组织结构，在分子性质预测、分子-表型检索和临床试验预测上取得更优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态分子表示学习方法仅优化跨模态对齐，忽略了化学空间的内在组织，导致分子表示扭曲和结构信息丢失。因此需要一种既能利用细胞表型信息进行对齐，又能保持分子结构邻近性的方法。

Method: PhenMol 将分子和细胞表征解耦为共享成分和私有成分，通过专用分子分支保留化学结构，实现表型引导的对齐而不破坏分子邻域组织。

Result: 在约 3.04×10⁴ 分子-细胞形态对上的实验表明，PhenMol 在 270 个生物活性任务上改善了分子性质预测、分子-表型检索和临床试验结果预测；ECFP4 结构分析显示其更好地保留了分子邻域并减少了嵌入失真。

Conclusion: 结构感知约束在多模态分子表示学习中至关重要，PhenMol 为整合细胞表型与化学知识进行药物发现提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Phenotypic drug discovery enables the discovery of functional relationships between molecular structures and cellular responses. However, existing multimodal representation learning methods often optimize cross-modal alignment without considering the intrinsic organization of chemical space, resulting in distorted molecular representations and loss of structural information. We propose \textbf{PhenMol}, a structure-preserving framework for phenotype-aware molecular representation learning. PhenMol disentangles molecular and cellular representations into shared and private components, enabling phenotype-guided alignment while preserving chemical structures through a dedicated molecular branch. This design integrates cellular phenotype information without disrupting molecular neighborhood organization. Experiments on approximately $3.04 \times 10^{4}$ molecule--cell morphology pairs demonstrate that PhenMol improves molecular property prediction across 270 bioactivity tasks, molecule--phenotype retrieval, and clinical trial outcome prediction. Moreover, ECFP4-based structural analysis shows that PhenMol better preserves molecular neighborhoods and reduces embedding distortion compared with existing multimodal alignment methods. These results highlight the importance of structure-aware constraints in multimodal molecular representation learning and provide an effective approach for integrating cellular phenotypes with chemical knowledge for drug discovery.

</details>


### [153] [GLOBE: Trajectory-Aligned Gradient Matching with Structured SparseOptimization for Coreset Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02690)
*Hetian Liu,Jin Cui,Mengcheng Shi,Yanbin Hu,Xinyue Long,Boran Zhao,Pengju Pen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GLOBE框架，通过将样本选择建模为全局优化的稀疏权重问题，利用跨多个训练检查点的梯度轨迹来捕捉样本在整个优化过程中的影响，并结合多阶匹配目标、结构化正则化和类别平衡选择，实现高效的核心集选择，在多个基准和架构上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于梯度的核心集选择方法通常依赖于单一模型快照的梯度，并使用贪心或追逐式选择策略，无法有效捕捉优化动态变化，也难以处理强相关样本，导致在低保留率下性能不佳。

Method: GLOBE将每个样本表示为跨多个训练检查点构建的梯度轨迹，引入多阶匹配目标（联合对齐一阶均值和投影无中心二阶矩）来保留全数据集的训练行为，结合Group LASSO、弹性网络正则化和非负预算约束实现组级和样本级稀疏性，最后通过类别平衡的Top-K选择保证类别覆盖。

Result: 在六个基准和五种评估架构上的实验表明，GLOBE在下游测试准确率上持续优于现有核心集选择方法，尤其在低保留率下优势显著。

Conclusion: 结合动态梯度信息、多阶分布匹配和结构化稀疏性可以有效提升数据效率学习，GLOBE为设备上训练提供了一种高效且准确的核心集选择方案。

Abstract: On-device training of deep neural networks is fundamentally constrained by the computational and memory costs of large-scale datasets. Coreset selection offers a practical solution by retaining only a compact subset of real training samples. However, existing gradient-based methods commonly rely on gradients computed at a single model snapshot and employ greedy or pursuit-based selection procedures, limiting their ability to capture evolving optimization dynamics and handle strongly correlated samples. We propose GLOBE (Gradient Local-Balanced Extraction), a trajectory-aligned coreset selection framework that formulates sample selection as a globally optimized sparse weighting problem. GLOBE represents each sample by a gradient trajectory constructed across multiple training checkpoints, thereby capturing its influence throughout different stages of optimization. To preserve the training behavior of the full dataset, we introduce a multi-order matching objective that jointly aligns the first-order mean and projected uncentered second-order moments of gradient trajectories. GLOBE further combines Group LASSO, Elastic Net regularization, and nonnegative budget constraints to induce group- and sample-level sparsity while stabilizing the weights of correlated trajectories. Finally, class-balanced Top-K selection maintains adequate category coverage under limited sampling budgets. Experiments across six benchmarks and five evaluation architectures demonstrate that GLOBE consistently outperforms existing coreset selection methods in downstream test accuracy, particularly at low retention ratios. These results highlight the effectiveness of combining dynamic gradient information, multi-order distribution matching, and structured sparsity for data-efficient learning.

</details>


### [154] [Output-Aware Rotation for INT2 KV-Cache Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02691)
*Vincent-Daniel Yun,Woosang Lim,Minsoo Cheong,Sunwoo Lee,Murali Annavaram,Sai Praneeth Karimireddy,Sungjoo Yoo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 OptR，一种面向输出的旋转量化方法，通过最小化后 $W_O$ 注意力输出误差来优化 KV 缓存 INT2 量化，在长上下文推理中同时改进 QuaRot 和 OSCAR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于旋转的 INT2 方法在完整注意力读出之前优化缓存统计量或代理误差，但模型最终受注意力传播和输出投影 $W_O$ 带来的误差影响，两者不匹配。

Method: 将后 $W_O$ 注意力输出误差分解为 key 和 value 诱导项，通过完整 INT2 量化与注意力路径学习每头正交校正；并应用注意力等价的 key 重参数化来减小大通道偏移而不改变 softmax 分布。

Result: 在三个模型、五个推理与编程基准上，OptR 一致优于 QuaRot 和 OSCAR，增强了长上下文检索能力，同时保持分页 KV 缓存格式且推理开销可忽略。

Conclusion: 面向输出的旋转量化能更有效地减小 KV 缓存 INT2 量化的实际推理误差，是一种即插即用的改进方案。

Abstract: The key-value (KV) cache has become a major memory and bandwidth bottleneck in long-context large language model inference, making ultra-low-bit quantization increasingly important. However, existing rotation-based INT2 methods optimize cache statistics or proxy errors before the complete attention readout, even though the model is ultimately affected by the error propagated through attention and the output projection $W_O$. To address this mismatch, we propose \textit{OptR}, an output-aware rotation method that minimizes post-$W_O$ attention-output error. OptR decomposes the post-$W_O$ attention-output error into key- and value-induced terms and learns per-head orthogonal corrections through the full INT2 quantization and attention path. OptR further applies an attention-equivalent key reparameterization to reduce large channel-wise offsets without changing the softmax distribution. Across three models and five reasoning and coding benchmarks, OptR consistently improves both QuaRot and OSCAR and strengthens long-context retrieval, while preserving the paged KV-cache format with negligible inference overhead.

</details>


### [155] [PatTree: a novel approach for automated creation of multimodal, graph-based patient representations for medical classification tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02692)
*Julia Gehrmann,Lars Quakulinski,Hamza Naseem,Oya Beyan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PatTree，一种基于图的患者整体表示，能够自动结构化多模态临床数据，无需预先标准化，实现早期数据集成，并在阿尔茨海默病分类中达到98.5%的平衡精度和0.987的F1分数，表明自动化结构化可作为临床AI管道的可扩展基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管多模态数据可提升AI在医疗分类中的性能，但临床真实数据的异质性（如缺失值、多时间点、多种模态、格式与语义不一致）给结构化分析和AI应用带来挑战。现有的数据协调方法资源密集且易错，限制了基于AI的决策支持的可扩展性和可重复性。

Method: 提出PatTree，一种基于图的患者整体表示，通过自动结构化多模态临床数据生成。它不需要预先标准化的输入，能够在统一的知识图谱中表示异质性临床数据，同时保留跨模态和数据源的语义关系，促进互操作性和机器可解释的数据访问。

Result: 在ADNI-1队列子集（n=763）上，使用PatTree直接进行三分类任务（区分阿尔茨海默病、轻度认知障碍和认知正常个体），在保留测试集上达到98.5%的平衡准确率和0.987的F1分数，达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: 无假设、自动化的多模态医学数据结构化可作为临床AI管道的可扩展基础，绕过繁琐的数据准备和标准化过程。

Abstract: Access to holistic, multimodal data improves the performance of Artificial Intelligence (AI) in medical classification tasks compared to utilizing single modalities or data sources. However, the inherent heterogeneity and complexity of clinical real-world data pose significant challenges to structured data analysis and AI application. This heterogeneity includes missing values, multiple time points, diverse modalities, and inconsistent formats and semantics. Data harmonization prior to data integration tackles this challenge but remains resource-intensive and error-prone, limiting the scalability and reproducibility of holistic, AI-driven decision support on clinical real-world data. We therefore propose PatTree, a graph-based, holistic representation of patients that can be derived from real-world clinical data through the automated structuring of multimodal clinical data. PatTree enables early-stage data integration without relying on pre-standardized inputs. While representing heterogeneous clinical data within a unified knowledge graph, PatTree preserves the semantic relationships between data elements across modalities and data sources, facilitating interoperability and machine-interpretable data access. Using a subset of the ADNI-1 cohort (n = 763), we demonstrate that classification of patients is directly feasible on PatTree reaching state-of-the-art classification performance. In the three-class classification task distinguishing Alzheimer's disease, mild cognitive impairment, and cognitively normal individuals, we achieve a balanced accuracy of 98.5% and an F$_1$ score of 0.987 on the held-out test set. Our results show that assumption-free, automated structuring of multimodal medical data can serve as a scalable foundation for clinical AI pipelines bypassing tedious data preparation and standardization.

</details>


### [156] [Measuring Explainer Stability via Attribution Separability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02697)
*Eddie Conti,Álvaro Parafita,Axel Brando*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于分布的框架来量化归因方法的稳定性，通过分析排序归因向量的可分离性确定可靠排名的最大索引，并扩展至跨数据集比较归因方法的排名鲁棒性，为评估可解释器稳定性提供了补充标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有归因方法因随机成分导致归因分数不稳定，缺乏系统评估其稳定性的框架。

Method: 提出一个基于分布的框架：首先通过分析排序归因向量的可分离性，确定特征排名保持可靠的最大索引；然后扩展该方法以比较不同归因方法在整个数据集上排名的鲁棒性。

Result: 实验展示了该方法如何应用于评估解释器的稳定性。

Conclusion: 该方法为评估归因方法的稳定性提供了一个互补的评判标准。

Abstract: Attribution methods (AMs) assign an importance score to each feature and are widely adopted to explain black-box models. However, most methods can produce variable attribution scores due to stochastic components in their definition. In this paper, we propose a distribution-based framework to capture the stability of attribution scores. In particular, our approach allows to understand the degree of separability in the ranked attribution vector and obtain the largest index for which a feature ranking remains reliable. We further extend this framework to compare AMs based on the robustness of their rankings across a dataset. Through experiments, we demonstrate how to apply our method to evaluate explainer stability. Overall, our approach provides a complementary criterion for evaluating the stability of AMs.

</details>


### [157] [NANQ: Noise-Floor-Aware Mixed-Precision Non-Uniform Quantization for Analog Compute-in-Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02700)
*Yizhe Chen,Wenshuai Yao,Saiya Wang,Yuannuo Feng,Wenbo Qi,Kechao Tang,Ngai Wong,Wenyong Zhou,Wang Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出NANQ，一种面向模拟存内计算(CIM)的噪声感知混合精度非均匀量化框架，通过对eFlash CIM阵列的实测噪声建模，将噪声分布转化为自适应量化密度并在噪声主导区域避免无效精度分配，在2-bit权重量化下比PowerQuant提升视觉模型准确率8.05个百分点、语言模型困惑度降低54.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟存内计算受器件变异和读取噪声影响，严重降低低比特量化模型的精度；现有CIM量化方法主要优化理想量化误差而忽视硬件噪声底限，导致精度分配效率低下。

Method: 1) 基于eFlash CIM阵列实测响应建立幅度相关的权重噪声模型；2) 将噪声分布转换为自适应量化密度，在低噪声区域分配更细分辨率，在噪声主导区域避免无效精度；3) 通过统一阈值识别每层在硬件噪声下的精度饱和点，分配逐层位宽。

Result: 在eFlash CIM SoC上进行片上实验，2-bit权重量化下：视觉模型准确率平均提升8.05个百分点；语言模型困惑度平均降低54.7%；混合精度NANQ仅用3.2-3.8等效比特即可获得大部分增益。

Conclusion: NANQ通过噪声感知的混合精度非均匀量化有效提升了模拟CIM在低比特下的部署精度，验证了硬件噪声建模指导量化策略的可行性。

Abstract: Analog compute-in-memory (CIM) enables energy-efficient neural network inference, but device variation and read noise can severely degrade low-bit quantized models. Existing CIM-oriented quantization methods mainly minimize ideal quantization error, ignoring the hardware noise floor and thus causing inefficient precision allocation. We propose NANQ, a noise-aware mixed-precision non-uniform quantization framework for analog CIM. NANQ models magnitude-dependent weight noise from measured responses of an eFlash CIM array and converts the noise profile into an adaptive quantization density, assigning finer resolution to low-noise regions while avoiding ineffective precision in noise-dominated regions. It further assigns layer-wise bit-widths by identifying each layer's precision saturation point under hardware noise using a unified threshold. On-chip experiments on an eFlash CIM SoC show that, under 2-bit weight-magnitude quantization, NANQ improves vision-model accuracy by 8.05 percentage points and reduces language-model PPL by 54.7% on average over PowerQuant. Mixed-precision NANQ captures most of the gains obtainable from additional quantization resources with only 3.2-3.8 equivalent bits.

</details>


### [158] [Can Training Logs Make Model Comparisons More Precise?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02705)
*Wei-Jung Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究如何利用训练日志（training logs）作为协变量进行组特异性协变量调整（arm-specific covariate adjustment），以提高随机训练模型之间性能比较的精度，并指出协变量选择带来的噪声是主要限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在比较随机训练模型时，需要估计性能差异及其不确定性。传统的重复运行比较方法精度有限，而训练日志中包含的统计信息可能有助于更精确地估计差异，但因其在训练过程中产生而非事先测量，需采用适当的调整方法。

Method: 采用组特异性协变量调整（arm-specific covariate adjustment），即每个模型仅使用自身运行中的统计量进行调整，保持原始均值差作为报告效应。在视觉任务上使用三种架构和三个数据集进行实验，利用早期训练日志中的简单统计量进行调整。

Result: 简单的早期训练日志调整通常能降低模型比较中的不确定性。但广泛搜索日志池中相关性最高的统计量往往会引入更多噪声，即使在事后看来存在有用的统计量。

Conclusion: 训练日志有助于更精确的模型比较，但调整方法必须避免大的选择噪声，否则会适得其反。

Abstract: Comparing stochastically trained models requires estimating both a performance difference and its uncertainty from repeated runs. We study whether training logs from those same runs can make such comparisons more precise. Because training-log covariates are produced during training rather than measured before it, we use arm-specific covariate adjustment: each model is adjusted only with statistics from its own runs, and the raw mean difference remains the reported effect. In a vision study spanning three architectures and three datasets, simple adjustments based on early training logs often reduce uncertainty in model comparisons. The main limitation is covariate selection. Broadly searching the log pool for the most correlated statistic often adds more noise than it removes, even when useful statistics exist in hindsight. Training logs therefore appear useful for more precise model comparisons, but only when the adjustment avoids large selection noise.

</details>


### [159] [Designing a Good Virtual Node: Addressable and Cardinality-Preserving Global Memory for Message Passing Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02709)
*Félix Marcoccia*

Main category: cs.LG
